Post kya kehta hai

  • @arungupta keynote se live: 'Kubernetes is a core component of AI inference', aur 'pichhle 8 mahine mein AI inference traffic 4x' (slide photos).
  • NVIDIA ab CNCF ka Platinum member; CoreWeave aur NVIDIA maintainers ko GPU credits de rahe hain; 'Open is the only way to solve some problems'.
  • CNCF ke naye sandbox projects: OpenShell, KAI scheduler (post sirf naam deta hai, details photo mein).
  • @kubernetesio/@K8sContributors: keynotes Salt Palace Hall DE mein live. @K8sArchitect RT (@K8sFM): Stephan ne bataya FluxCD v1 growing adoption ke saath keep up nahi kar pa raha tha, isliye unhone Flux v2 rewrite lead kiya. @alexxubyte: 'Kubernetes Explained for Busy Engineers' infographic.

Deep analysis

  • Inference K8s pe kyun: GPU pods ko same control plane (scheduling, autoscaling, rollout, observability) milta hai jo baaki services ko. Par default scheduler GPU-aware nahi jitna chahiye: fractional GPU, gang scheduling (saare pods ek saath ya koi nahi), queue/quota fairness — isi gap ke liye KAI jaisa batch/AI scheduler aata hai (NVIDIA ne ise Run:ai codebase se 2025 mein open-source kiya tha).
  • 4x traffic ka matlab: cost ab GPU utilization pe tika hai. Isi Page ke llm-d (cache-aware routing) aur Go GOMAXPROCS cards se jodo: right-sizing + smart routing = GPU bill control.
  • GPU credits for maintainers: open source AI infra projects ko CI/testing ke liye GPUs chahiye, jo maintainers ke paas aam taur pe nahi hote.
  • Flux v1 → v2 lesson: adoption badhne pe architecture limit hit hui, to rewrite kiya (v2 GitOps Toolkit controllers). Trade-off: rewrite = migration pain users ke liye, par long-term scale.

Mini example (K8s: GPU inference pod request)

apiVersion: apps/v1 kind: Deployment metadata: { name: llm-infer } spec: replicas: 2 selector: { matchLabels: { app: llm-infer } } template: metadata: { labels: { app: llm-infer } } spec: containers: - name: server image: my-registry/vllm-server:1.0 resources: limits: { nvidia.com/gpu: 1 } # device plugin se GPU readinessProbe: { httpGet: { path: /health, port: 8000 } }

FDE / interview angle

  • Client bole 'humein inference self-host karna hai' to K8s + GPU device plugin + GPU-aware scheduler + autoscaling on queue depth/latency ka design bata sako.
  • Sawaal: CPU HPA GPU workloads pe kyun kaam nahi karta? Jawab: bottleneck GPU memory/queue hai, CPU nahi; custom metrics (tokens/sec, queue length) pe scale karo.

Self-check

  1. Gang scheduling kya hai aur distributed training/inference ke liye default kube-scheduler kyun kaafi nahi?
  2. Inference traffic 4x hone pe cost control ke teen levers batao.
Agents ko tools do, pixels nahi: FreeSQL WebMCP + Adobe MCP + AI token budgetsHistorical@OracleDevs @tldrnewsletter @jbaruch · AI / Agents & LLMs · 10:02 PM IST (10 Nov)Browser agent DOM click karne ki jagah page-registered tools use kare (existing session, koi credential agent ko nahi); Adobe MCP preview; Finance ke liye token budget guardrail.

@OracleDevs @tldrnewsletter @jbaruch · AI/Agents · 10:02 PM IST (10 Nov)

Sources: https://x.com/OracleDevs/status/2120184709284053194 · https://x.com/tldrnewsletter/status/2120180948637507879 · https://x.com/jbaruch/status/2120181274904232203

The tools run in the page and use the session you already have. No credentials handed to the agent. No wallet. No connect string.— @OracleDevs

Post kya kehta hai

  • @OracleDevs: browser agents impressive hain par galat bhi ho sakte hain. @krisrice demo: FreeSQL WebMCP tools register karta hai, taaki agent direct page manipulation ki jagah un tools ko use kare.
  • @tldrnewsletter: Adobe ne MCP support preview kiya jisse bahar ke AI agents Photoshop aur Premiere project files edit kar sakein; private beta early 2027.
  • @jbaruch (AI DevCon NYC talk): 'I'll give you more tokens, but first explain what happened and how much more you need.' Bill pe koi fire nahi hota; budget model off tha, aur Finance ko 'ruko, baat karo, saath decide karenge' kehne ka tareeka chahiye.

Deep analysis

  • DOM clicking vs tools: screenshot/DOM se agent ko UI 'guess' karna padta hai (button kahan hai, kya load hua), jo layout change pe toot jaata hai. Page khud typed tools (naam, input schema, output) expose kare to agent ka action deterministic aur validate-able ho jaata hai.
  • Security: tool page ke andar user ke existing session mein chalta hai, isliye agent ko password/DB connect string nahi milta. Isi Page ke Railway aur LLMJacking cards dikhate hain ki agent/CI ke paas pade credentials hi attack target bante hain.
  • Trade-off: app owner ko tools likhne/maintain karne padte hain, aur tool scope galat ho to agent zyada kar sakta hai; read vs write tools alag rakho aur destructive tools pe user confirmation.
  • Adobe MCP: creative apps bhi agent-callable ban rahe hain; MCP ek common protocol hai jisse ek agent bahut saare apps ke tools discover kare.
  • Token budgets: agent loops cost unpredictable bana dete hain. jbaruch ka point governance hai: hard cap pe stop, explanation + estimate ke saath escalation, phir decision — FinOps for AI.

Mini example (Spring AI: agent ko narrow tool do + token budget guard)

@Component class OrderTools { private final OrderService orders; // user ke session/tenant se scoped @Tool(description = "Get status of one order owned by the current user") OrderStatus orderStatus(String orderId) { return orders.statusForCurrentUser(orderId); // koi raw SQL/connect string agent ko nahi } } class TokenBudget { private final AtomicLong used = new AtomicLong(); private final long cap; TokenBudget(long cap) { this.cap = cap; } void charge(long tokens) { if (used.addAndGet(tokens) > cap) throw new BudgetExceededException("explain usage + estimate, then ask for more"); } }

FDE / interview angle

  • Client ka browser-automation agent flaky hai? Pehla suggestion: critical flows ke liye app-side tools/API expose karo, scraping last resort.
  • 'Agent ko credentials kaise doge?' ka strong jawab: nahi doge — user session/short-lived scoped tokens, tool-level authorization, audit log.
  • Enterprise rollout mein per-team token budget, alerts aur escalation path batana FDE ko trust dilata hai.

Self-check

  1. Browser agent ke DOM manipulation aur page-registered tools mein reliability aur security ka farak samjhao.
  2. Agent ke token spend ko kaise cap karoge bina user ka kaam beech mein bigade?
CNCF Q3 2026 report: 21.2M cloud-native devs (41%), 75% AI se code ship karte hain + naye sandbox/graduated projectsHistorical@NaveenS16 (→ The New Stack) · DevOps / K8s / Cloud · 9:17 AM IST (11 Nov)Cloud-native devs 75% vs non-cloud-native ~50% AI use; KubeVirt, Karmada, KubeFlow graduate; KubeRay, Agent Substrate, OpenShell, OpenRL sandbox mein.

@NaveenS16 → The New Stack · DevOps/Cloud · 9:17 AM IST (11 Nov)

Sources: https://x.com/NaveenS16/status/2120350019723362552 → https://thenewstack.io/cncf-developer-ai-adoption/ (Alex Wilhelm, 10 Nov 2026) · thread: https://x.com/NaveenS16/status/2120351430653681739 · https://x.com/NaveenS16/status/2120353551172210987 · https://x.com/NaveenS16/status/2120355161302528381

they’re looking for an edge, they’re looking for an opportunity to work on the latest things and really push the envelope— Jonathan Bryce, CNCF (via The New Stack)

Post kya kehta hai

KubeCon NA (Salt Lake City) pe CNCF ki State of Cloud Native Development report aayi. Naveen ke 4 posts: 21.2 million cloud-native devs = sab software engineers ka 41%+; 75% cloud-native devs AI se code ship karte hain; wo AI pe jaldi isliye kude kyunki 'edge' dhoondhte hain; aur zyada log development roles mein aa rahe hain (@jbryce).

Deep analysis (article se)

  • Numbers: Q3 2026 tak 21.2M cloud-native developers (41% of total). Non-cloud-native devs mein ~half AI se code mein help lete hain, cloud-native mein three-quarters. AI use karne waalon mein just under two-thirds ke liye AI workflow ka central/core hissa hai.
  • CNCF maturity ladder: sandbox (yahan project 'rigorous processes' banata hai taaki trustworthy aur predictable ho) → incubation → graduation. ~250 projects mein se sirf handful graduate hote hain.
  • Naye sandbox: KubeRay, Agent Substrate, OpenShell, OpenRL (aur others). Naye incubation: Meshery, K8GB. Graduating: KubeVirt, Karmada, KubeFlow. Interview mein podcast ne Kubernetes AI Conformance program bhi cover kiya.
  • Kyun matter karta hai: AI/agent workloads ka infra layer (Ray on K8s, agent runtimes, ML pipelines) CNCF ke andar standardize ho raha hai — Agent Infra aur KubeCon keynote cards wali story ka data side.
  • Trade-off / caveat: yeh survey-based self-reported numbers hain; 'AI se ship' ka matlab har jagah same nahi. Sandbox project = early, production pe lagane se pehle maturity aur maintainers check karo.
  • Bryce ka point: kisi bhi org mein ho, open source tech samjho kyunki wo company ko 'leverage options' deta hai (vendor lock-in ke against).

FDE / interview angle

Client stack choose karte waqt CNCF stage ek quick risk signal hai: graduated (KubeVirt, Karmada, KubeFlow) = production adoption + governance proven; sandbox (KubeRay, Agent Substrate) = PoC ke liye theek, par fallback plan rakho. FDE role mein 'AI adoption' ki baat data se karo: 75% vs ~50% gap batata hai ki cloud-native teams AI tooling jaldi lengi, toh unke paas pehle guardrails (review, CI checks) chahiye.

Self-check

  1. CNCF sandbox, incubation aur graduation stages mein kya farak hai, aur client ko sandbox project recommend karte waqt kya check karoge?
  2. Report ke hisaab se cloud-native aur non-cloud-native devs mein AI adoption kitna alag hai, aur Bryce iska kya reason deta hai?
Effect Oriented Programming: effects (network, user input, failures) ko type system mein lao — agent-first languages isi tarafHistorical@JamesWard · Java / Spring / JPA · 10:20 AM IST (11 Nov)Eckel/Frasure/Ward ki book ka idea: unpredictable parts ko Effect System se control karo; EffectTS, foldkit, alchemy_run jaise projects mainstream ho rahe.

@JamesWard · Programming paradigms · 10:20 AM IST (11 Nov)

Source: https://x.com/JamesWard/status/2120375933370540285 → https://effectorientedprogramming.com/

Many new Agent-First programming languages are Effect Oriented.— @JamesWard

Post kya kehta hai

James Ward: do saal pehle unhone, Bruce Eckel aur Bill Frasure ne 'Effect Oriented Programming' book nikali. Ab programming ki duniya badal gayi, par concept pakad raha hai — kai naye Agent-First languages effect-oriented hain, aur @EffectTS_, @foldkit, @alchemy_run jaise projects 'masses' tak pahunch rahe. Wo claim nahi karte ki ye book ki wajah se hai.

Deep analysis

  • Book site ki definition: 'Effects are the unpredictable elements in your programs' — network communication, user interaction jaise concerns. Algorithms ki clean duniya failures aur inconsistency mein badal jaati hai.
  • Site ka claim: traditionally hum effects ko 'incompletely and often unwittingly' handle karte aaye; Effect Systems se in unpredictable parts ko control karo aur resilient systems banao.
  • Under the hood (general idea): function seedha side effect nahi karta, balki ek 'description' (value) return karta hai jisme likha hota hai ki kya effect chahiye, kaunsi failure aa sakti hai, kaunsi dependency chahiye. Runtime baad mein usse execute karta hai — isliye retry, timeout, fallback, testing mein fake dependency sab composable ho jaate hain.
  • Agents se link kyun: LLM-generated code mein sabse risky hissa side effects hain (API calls, file writes, payments). Agar effects type/signature mein dikhte hain toh reviewer aur tooling dekh sakte hain ki code kya chhoo sakta hai — yeh post ka 'agent-first languages effect oriented hain' wala point samajhne ka ek tareeka hai (yeh meri analysis hai, post ne mechanism nahi bataya).
  • Trade-offs: learning curve, naya vocabulary, stack traces/debugging thode alag; team mein sab ko same style chahiye. Chhote CRUD service mein overhead zyada lag sakta hai; jahan failures/retries/concurrency zyada hain (payments, integrations) wahan fayda dikhta hai.

Mini example (Java shape: effect ko value banao)

// Side effect turant nahi chalta; pehle ek "description" banti hai sealed interface Result<T> permits Ok, Err {} record Ok<T>(T value) implements Result<T> {} record Err<T>(String reason) implements Result<T> {} // Supplier = effect ka description; failure type signature mein dikhta hai Supplier<Result<Rate>> fetchFx = () -> { try { return new Ok<>(fxClient.rate("USD", "INR")); } catch (IOException e) { return new Err<>("fx-down"); } }; // Composition: retry policy alag se lagao, business logic touch kiye bina Result<Rate> r = retry(fetchFx, 3); // test mein fetchFx ko fake se swap karo

FDE / interview angle

Client integration (FX rate, payment gateway, Kafka publish) mein interview ya design review pe poocha ja sakta hai: 'failures kahan handle karoge?' Effect-oriented soch se jawab do: har external call ek explicit effect hai jiska failure type, timeout aur retry policy declare hai — Spring mein yeh Resilience4j + typed result objects se approximate hota hai. Agent-generated code review karte waqt bhi pehle effects dekho: kaun sa code network/DB/payment chhoota hai.

Self-check

  1. Book site ke hisaab se 'effect' kya hai, aur effect ko turant chalane ki jagah 'description' return karne ka kya fayda hai?
  2. Payments service mein checked exceptions vs typed Result/Effect approach ke trade-offs kya honge?
Database scaling ke 10 concepts: index se materialized view tak (+ large-file upload, 100 APIs ka dard)Historical@ashishps_1 @freeCodeCamp @NaveenS16 · System design & distributed · 11:20 AM IST (11 Nov)Indexing, vertical scaling, caching, sharding, replication, query optimization, connection pooling, vertical partitioning, denormalization, materialized views — kab kaunsa lever.

@ashishps_1 @freeCodeCamp @NaveenS16 · System design / Databases · 11:20 AM IST (11 Nov)

Source: https://x.com/ashishps_1/status/2120383369490096471 (AlgoMaster System Design links, e.g. https://algomaster.io/learn/system-design/indexing)

Post kya kehta hai

  1. Indexing: frequently accessed columns pe index banao, read queries fast.
  2. Vertical scaling: DB server pe zyada CPU, RAM ya storage.
  3. Caching: Redis jaise in-memory store se hot data serve karo, DB load kam.
  4. Sharding: DB ko chhote independent shards mein todo, servers pe distribute karo (horizontal scaling).
  5. Replication: multiple replicas, read traffic balance + availability.
  6. Query optimization: SQL tune karo, expensive operations hatao, indexes sahi use karo.
  7. Connection pooling: connections reuse karo, open/close overhead bachao.
  8. Vertical partitioning: bade table ko columns ke subsets mein todo.
  9. Denormalization: redundant par structured data rakho taaki complex joins kam hon (read-heavy).
  10. Materialized views: complex query ka result pre-compute karke alag table mein store karo.

Deep analysis

  • Order matters (meri analysis): pehle sasta aur reversible lever — query optimization, index, connection pool — phir cache aur read replicas, sabse aakhir mein sharding. Sharding schema, transactions aur ops sab badal deta hai, isliye last resort.
  • Index trade-off: B-tree index reads fast karta hai par har INSERT/UPDATE pe index bhi update hota hai, aur storage lagta hai. Write-heavy table pe zyada indexes = write latency.
  • Replication = eventual consistency risk: async replica pe abhi likha data turant nahi dikhta. Read-your-writes chahiye toh woh read primary pe bhejo (Spring mein read-only routing card dekho: rwroute).
  • Cache invalidation asli problem hai: cache-aside + TTL simple hai par stale reads deta hai; payments balance jaise data pe cache mat karo ya very short TTL + write pe evict.
  • Sharding key choose karna sabse critical: hot keys (ek bada merchant) ek shard ko overload karte hain; cross-shard joins/transactions mehenge. Account-id jaisa high-cardinality key common choice.
  • Materialized view vs denormalization: dono read ke liye write-time ya refresh-time pe kaam karte hain; MV refresh stale ho sakta hai (Postgres mein REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY ke liye unique index chahiye).

Mini example (Spring Boot: pool + index + cache)

# application.yml — HikariCP pool (Spring Boot default) spring.datasource.hikari: maximum-pool-size: 20 # DB max_connections / app instances se size karo connection-timeout: 3000 # pool khaali ho to fast fail @Entity @Table(indexes = @Index(name = "idx_txn_account_created", columnList = "accountId, createdAt")) // composite: filter + sort class Txn { @Id Long id; String accountId; Instant createdAt; BigDecimal amount; } @Cacheable(cacheNames = "fxRates", key = "#pair") // Redis cache-aside, TTL config mein public Rate rate(String pair) { return repo.findLatest(pair); }

FDE / interview angle

Client bole 'DB slow hai, shard kar do' — FDE ka jawab: pehle data lao (slow query log, EXPLAIN plan, pool wait time, read/write ratio), phir sasta fix. Interview mein 10 concepts list karna kaafi nahi; har ek ka cost batao (index = write cost, replica = staleness, shard = cross-shard txn) aur payments jaise strong-consistency flow mein kaunsa avoid karoge.

Self-check

  1. Read-heavy reporting dashboard ke liye materialized view, denormalized table aur read replica mein se kaise choose karoge?
  2. Composite index (accountId, createdAt) kis query ko help karta hai aur kis query ko nahi (column order kyun matter karta hai)?

Quick picks (same run)

  • @freeCodeCamp (https://x.com/freeCodeCamp/status/2120381295859495194): @reetain_raina ka tutorial — large file upload karke download link share karne mein kya lagta hai: chunk management, object storage, encryption (https://www.freecodecamp.org/news/how-file-transfer-services-work/). Pattern (general): file ko chunks mein todo, har chunk retry-able, object storage (S3 multipart jaisa) mein, phir time-limited link.
  • @NaveenS16 (https://x.com/NaveenS16/status/2120393803198599571): 100 APIs manage karna matlab 1,000 alag headaches — itne endpoints ko healthy, secure aur breaking-change-free rakhna operational chaos hai. Iska standard jawab: API versioning, contract tests, gateway pe central auth/rate-limit.
Quarkus 4 HTTP/3: ek REST endpoint, do transports (QUIC over UDP) + Java/frontend tool picksHistorical@myfear @vlad_mihalcea @InfoQ · Java / Spring / JPA · 1:15 PM IST (11 Nov)Same Jakarta REST method HTTP/1.1, /2, /3 serve karta hai; HTTP/3 ko UDP 8443 mapping + Linux-matching native quiche lib chahiye.

@myfear · Java / Quarkus / Networking · 1:15 PM IST (11 Nov)

Source: https://x.com/myfear/status/2120419481977487476 · Article: https://www.the-main-thread.com/p/quarkus-4-http3-quic-udp (Markus Eisele, Quarkus 4.0.0.Beta1, Java 21; HTTP/3 support experimental, final Quarkus 4 end of Nov 2026 planned)

Post kya kehta hai

  • quarkus-http3 extension se ek REST endpoint banaya; HTTP/1.1, HTTP/2 aur HTTP/3 teeno same Java method tak pahunchte hain (method request.version() return karta hai: HTTP_1_1 / HTTP_2 / HTTP_3).
  • Podman mein sirf TCP 8443 publish kiya to HTTP/2 chala, HTTP/3 fail — HTTP/3 ko UDP 8443 bhi publish karna padta hai (TCP aur UDP ke port spaces alag hain).
  • Mac (Apple Silicon) pe build kiya jar Linux ARM container mein crash: libnetty_quiche42_linux_aarch_64.so missing. Fix: netty-codec-native-quic dependency with classifier linux-aarch_64 (amd64 ke liye linux-x86_64).
  • Beta1 gotchas article ke mutabiq: quarkus dev mode mein HTTP/3 request pe ClassCastException (500), isliye packaged jar use kiya; HTTP/2 response pe Alt-Svc header nahi aaya jabki guide kehta hai aana chahiye.

Deep analysis

  • HTTP/2 multiplexing karta hai par sab streams ek TCP byte stream mein — ek packet loss pe TCP baaki sab streams ka data rok leta hai (transport head-of-line blocking). HTTP/3 har request ko alag QUIC stream pe bhejta hai; QUIC ordering per-stream handle karta hai, to unrelated streams chalte rehte hain (congestion control phir bhi shared).
  • QUIC UDP ke upar reliability, flow control, congestion control, loss recovery khud deta hai, aur TLS 1.3 handshake mein built-in hai — isliye HTTPS mandatory. Prior state ho to 0-RTT early data possible, par replay risk ke saath.
  • Discovery vs negotiation: Alt-Svc header (h3=":8443"; ma=86400) client ko batata hai ki HTTP/3 available hai (24h cache); ALPN connection banate waqt protocol (h2/h3) choose karta hai. Header compression: HTTP/2 HPACK, HTTP/3 QPACK.
  • Deployment trade-off: UDP path end-to-end chahiye — container ports, firewall, K8s Service, load balancer/proxy. Agar proxy HTTP/3 terminate karke HTTP/2 forward kare to app 'HTTP_2' dekhega. TCP HTTPS fallback hamesha rakho kyunki kai networks UDP block karte hain. Port translate karo to Alt-Svc sahi (client-reachable) port advertise kare.
  • Java portable hai par native QUIC lib OS+arch specific hai — multi-arch images mein classifier dependencies ka dhyan. Author ne benchmark nahi kiya; speed gain workload, latency aur packet loss pe depend karta hai.

Mini example (article se, Quarkus REST + Podman)

@Path("/protocol") public class ProtocolResource { @GET @Produces(MediaType.TEXT_PLAIN) public String protocol(io.vertx.core.http.HttpServerRequest req) { return req.version().name(); // HTTP_1_1 | HTTP_2 | HTTP_3 } } # application.properties (prod profile, TLS registry) %prod.quarkus.tls.key-store.pem.0.cert=${HTTP3_CERTIFICATE:/certs/localhost.pem} %prod.quarkus.tls.key-store.pem.0.key=${HTTP3_PRIVATE_KEY:/certs/localhost-key.pem} podman run -p 127.0.0.1:8443:8443/tcp -p 127.0.0.1:8443:8443/udp ... localhost/http3-demo curl -k --fail --http3-only https://localhost:8443/protocol # -> HTTP_3

FDE / interview angle

Client bole 'HTTP/3 enable kiya par kaam nahi kar raha' — FDE checklist: UDP port har hop pe open hai? (container, security group, LB), runtime ke liye sahi native lib hai?, Alt-Svc client-reachable port bata raha hai?, aur curl --http3-only se test (plain --http3 chupke se HTTP/2 pe fallback kar leta hai). Interview mein HOL blocking TCP vs QUIC ka difference 2 line mein bol paana strong signal hai; mobile/lossy networks (payments app) pe yeh matter karta hai.

Self-check

  1. HTTP/2 multiplexing ke baad bhi head-of-line blocking kyun bachta hai, aur QUIC ise kaise hatata hai?
  2. Alt-Svc aur ALPN mein kya fark hai — kaun discovery karta hai aur kaun protocol select karta hai?

Quick picks (same run)

(Tool JPA/Hibernate mapping aur config issues detect karta hai — Spring Data JPA projects ke liye useful.)
  • @InfoQ (https://x.com/InfoQ/status/2120414962451669473): 'Angular's TypeScript 7-Powered Compiler Enters Beta with Rust-Based Preprocessor' by Daniel Curtis (https://bit.ly/4iMD5Wn) — sirf headline; details article mein.
Model routing study (Route Anything): routers ka asli fayda cost hai, quality jump nahiHistorical@myfear (RT @rohanpaul_ai) · AI / Agents & LLMs · 1:15 PM IST (11 Nov)452 models/policies, 25 benchmarks: avg 8.82x quality per cost, <0.5 point loss; learned router sirf +0.3 vs oracle +5.4.

@myfear RT @rohanpaul_ai · AI / LLMs · 1:15 PM IST (11 Nov)

Source: https://x.com/myfear/status/2120420144048488478 (original https://x.com/rohanpaul_ai/status/2120209275515736120)

Post kya kehta hai

  • UC Berkeley + other labs ki study 'Route Anything': 452 candidate models aur policies ko same inputs pe 25 benchmarks pe chalaya (text QA se robot control tak); 374,401 scored runs pe routers train kiye, quality aur cost dono measure kiye.
  • Routers average 8.82x more quality per unit cost dete hain, 0.5 point se kam quality loss ke saath.
  • Perfect per-input (oracle) router best single model ko 5.4 points se beat karta, par learned routers 16 benchmarks pe sirf 0.3 gain kar paaye.
  • Coding tasks pe routing ne hamesha Opus 4.6 call karne ke muqable 25.5x dollar efficiency di, 0.44-point loss pe. Advice: routers ko mainly kaam sasta model pe shift karne ke liye use karo jab quality close ho, aur gains trust karne se pehle candidates ko same inputs pe compare karo.

Deep analysis

  • Router = ek chhota classifier/policy jo har request ke liye model choose karta hai (cheap vs frontier). Oracle gap (5.4 vs 0.3) batata hai ki 'kaunsa model is input pe best hoga' predict karna mushkil hai — par 'kya sasta model kaafi hai' predict karna aasaan aur paisa bachata hai.
  • Isliye practical design: default cheap model, sirf hard/uncertain inputs escalate (cascade/fallback). Trade-off: router ki apni latency + misroute pe quality drop; eval set pe same inputs ke saath A/B karna zaroori (study ka bhi yahi advice).
  • Production angle (meri analysis): router AI gateway layer mein baithta hai — cost budgets, per-tenant policy, aur telemetry (kaunsa model, kitna cost, quality score) ke saath.

Mini example (Python: cost-first router shape)

def route(prompt: str) -> str: # cheap model default; escalate only when router is unsure / task is hard p_hard = difficulty_clf.predict_proba([prompt])[0][1] return "frontier-model" if p_hard > 0.7 else "small-model" # evaluate on SAME inputs: quality and $ per model vs router for x in eval_set: log(x.id, route(x.prompt), score(...), cost(...))

FDE / interview angle

Client ka LLM bill bada hai — FDE pitch: pehle traffic ka eval set banao, har request pe small vs large model ka quality/cost measure karo, phir router ya simple rules se 'close quality' wale cases sasta model pe bhejo. 'Router se quality badhegi' wala promise mat karo; study ke mutabiq asli jeet cost mein hai.

Self-check

  1. Oracle router aur learned router ke beech 5.4 vs 0.3 ka gap kya batata hai?
  2. Spring AI app mein cheap-first + escalate routing kahan implement karoge, aur kaunsi metrics log karoge?

Metastable failures: jab retry code hi outage ban jaye + Cloudflare D1 control plane (millions of DBs)Historical@javacodegeeks @InfoQ · System design & distributed · 8:16 AM IST (11 Nov)Retries load 700→2,800/s kar dete hain aur trigger khatam hone ke baad bhi system gira rehta hai; fix = retry budget + jitter + ek layer pe retry + load shedding.

@javacodegeeks @InfoQ · System design · 8:16 AM IST (11 Nov)

Sources: https://x.com/javacodegeeks/status/2120347056464072764 (→ https://www.javacodegeeks.com/2026/11/metastable-failures-and-retry-storms-when-your-resilience-code-is-the-outage.html) · https://x.com/InfoQ/status/2120341747674804278 (→ InfoQ 'Cloudflare Scales D1 Control Plane to Millions of Databases')

Treat retry code as load you are adding, not as free safety.— Java Code Geeks

Post kya kehta hai

  • Metastable failure = outage jo khud ko zinda rakhta hai: trigger (load spike / capacity drop) khatam ho jaye phir bhi sustaining effect system ko gira ke rakhta hai. Framework Bronson et al. 2021, follow-up OSDI 2022 (Huang et al.).
  • OSDI study: 11 orgs se 22 metastable failures; pichhle decade ke 15 major AWS outages mein kam se kam 4 isi type ke; aadhe se zyada incidents mein sustaining effect retry policy thi.
  • Article ki apni simulation (simplified, benchmark nahi): server 1,000 rps, clients 700 rps, 10 sec capacity 400. Unlimited 3 retries se load 2,800 rps tak, 90 sec mein recover nahi; 10% retry budget se peak 849 rps aur second 33 tak recover.
  • D1 (InfoQ): control plane Postgres (metadata source of truth) + ClickHouse (analytics) + Workers KV (globally replicated routing). Daily active databases ek saal mein 18x.

Deep analysis

  • Retries 'selfish' hain (Marc Brooker, Builders' Library): har client apni success ke liye server ka time kharch karta hai, total ko koi coordinate nahi karta. 3 layers x 3 retries = 4x4x4 = 64 attempts bottom pe ek user request ke liye.
  • Server FIFO queue mein un requests pe time waste karta hai jinke clients already timeout karke chale gaye — goodput zero, throughput full. Isliye 'server busy hai' aur 'server useful kaam kar raha hai' alag cheezein hain.
  • Fixes: (1) retry budget (token bucket — AWS SDK mein 2016 se), (2) capped exponential backoff + jitter (budget ke saath, akela nahi), (3) sirf ek layer retry kare, (4) load shed karo / jo request ka client ja chuka use drop karo, (5) sirf idempotent calls retry karo. Builders' Library circuit breakers ke modal behaviour ko test karna mushkil maanti hai, isliye local retry limits prefer.
  • D1 mein yahi soch: 30-sec heartbeats per-database jitter ke saath har Durable Object ko Postgres se reconcile karte hain, failed create/delete self-correct; heartbeat thoda stale data tolerate karta hai to cache / read replica se serve. Workers KV durable store hai (sirf cache nahi) — Cloudflare khud likhta hai ki catastrophic cache purge mein million databases routing fetch karte to metastable failure hota.
  • Storage limits: async usage updates queue + batch, pre-aggregated account totals incremental deltas se — Postgres ko write spikes aur scans se bachana. Trade-off: limit enforcement thoda eventually consistent.

Mini example (Java: retry budget + full jitter)

final class RetryBudget { // har request 0.1 token kamaati hai => ~10% retries private final double ratio, cap = 100; private double tokens = 10; RetryBudget(double ratio) { this.ratio = ratio; } synchronized void onRequest() { tokens = Math.min(cap, tokens + ratio); } synchronized boolean tryRetry() { if (tokens >= 1) { tokens -= 1; return true; } return false; } } <T> T call(Supplier<T> op, RetryBudget budget) throws InterruptedException { budget.onRequest(); for (int attempt = 0; ; attempt++) { try { return op.get(); } // op idempotent hona chahiye catch (TimeoutException_ e) { if (attempt >= 2 || !budget.tryRetry()) throw e; // budget khatam => fail fast long capMs = 2_000, baseMs = 100; Thread.sleep(ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextLong(Math.min(capMs, baseMs << attempt))); } } }

Note: TimeoutException_ placeholder hai (unchecked wrapper). Resilience4j Retry backoff + jitter deta hai; budget ka logic custom ya gateway/service mesh level pe lagao.

FDE / interview angle

  • Payments/interbank context: client 'retry badha do' bole to pehle poocho — call idempotent hai? (idempotency key), kitni layers retry kar rahi hain (gateway + service + SDK)? Double debit aur retry storm dono yahin se aate hain (multi-rail failover card dekho).
  • Incident review mein trigger aur sustaining effect alag likho: 'DB slow hua' trigger, 'retries ne 4x load rakha' sustaining. Fix sirf trigger pe karoge to agla spike phir gira dega.
  • System design answer mein bolo: control plane data plane se alag (D1 query path Postgres ko touch nahi karta), reconciliation loops with jitter, durable routing store.

Self-check

  1. Backoff + jitter akela retry storm kyun nahi rokta, aur retry budget kya extra guarantee deta hai?
  2. D1 ne heartbeats mein per-database jitter kyun daala, aur Workers KV ko sirf cache rakhte to cache purge pe kya hota?
Agent distraction: stale TODOs coding agents ko side-quests pe bhejte hain (Google) + brownfield code imitation + Claude Code hygieneHistorical@InfoQ @AdamBien @karimov_elshad · AI / Agents & LLMs · 8:16 AM IST (11 Nov)Detour wale sessions mein 70% zyada steps, 77% zyada tokens; comments bhi context hain, codebase saaf = agent sasta.

@InfoQ @AdamBien @karimov_elshad · AI/Agents · 8:16 AM IST (11 Nov)

Sources: https://x.com/InfoQ/status/2120218592541376886 (→ InfoQ, Sergio De Simone) · https://x.com/AdamBien/status/2120259661626765725 · https://x.com/karimov_elshad/status/2120343253027623332 · https://x.com/karimov_elshad/status/2120254544139755981 · https://x.com/karimov_elshad/status/2120297954556710913

Obsolete specs, long resolved bugs, and lagging documentation ... are unintentionally ingested as context indirecting and misdirecting the next wave of coders - the agents.— Google (via InfoQ)

Post kya kehta hai

  • Google (Sam Saccone, Madhuri Garikapati): purane TODO(bug/...) comments, obsolete bug refs, outdated specs agents ko unrequested side-quests pe le jaate hain.
  • Hundreds of millions agent steps ke traces: ~13% sessions mein agent inline TODO/FIXME dekhta hai; unmein 6% mein us bug pe kaam shuru kar deta hai, aksar prompt mein bug ka zikr bhi nahi. Detours mein 41% already closed tickets, 57% open par scope ke bahar (scope creep).
  • Cost: detour sessions mein 70% zyada steps, 77% zyada tokens, 105% zyada wait time. Fix: scanner agents jo git history dekh ke confirm karte hain TODO obsolete hai, phir PR — 6 mahine mein hazaron refs hataye, 95%+ human acceptance.
  • @AdamBien 'Brownfield Templates: The Model Imitates What It Finds' (Screaming Java for Agents ep. 2, https://youtu.be/ur4r12eBnUY): model codebase mein jo pattern dekhta hai wahi copy karta hai.
  • @karimov_elshad Claude Code tips: /doctor setup check karta hai (broken/duplicate installs, unused skills/MCP/plugins aur unka context cost, CLAUDE.md ki wo lines jo code se samajh aa sakti hain); baar-baar wale instructions ko skill banao jo sirf zarurat pe load ho; messy repo ke liye 7 prompts thread.

Deep analysis

  • Agent ke liye comment = instruction-jaisa context. Human senior dev stale TODO ignore kar deta hai kyunki usse history pata hai; agent ke paas wo prior nahi, isliye 'helpful' banke chase karta hai. Ek reply ne kaha: junior dev behaviour at machine speed.
  • Google ka method interesting hai: har TODO ko bug status + user prompt se cross-reference karke valid kaam vs detour automatically classify — yani agent evals production traces se, lab benchmark se nahi.
  • Brownfield imitation same coin ka doosra side: agar repo mein bad pattern zyada hai to agent use replicate karega. Isliye cleanup (TODOs, dead code, purane docs) ab hygiene nahi, agent cost optimisation hai.
  • Context budget: unused MCP servers/skills bhi har session mein tokens khaate hain; /doctor jaisi audit aur on-demand skills isi problem ka tool-side fix. Trade-off: cleanup agents ke PRs bhi review load badhate hain — isliye high-precision (git history verify) zaroori.

Mini example (Python: closed bugs wale TODOs dhoondo)

import re, pathlib TODO = re.compile(r"TODO\(bug/(\d+)\)") def stale_todos(root, is_closed): # is_closed: bug tracker API call for p in pathlib.Path(root).rglob("*.java"): for n, line in enumerate(p.read_text(errors="ignore").splitlines(), 1): m = TODO.search(line) if m and is_closed(m.group(1)): yield f"{p}:{n} -> bug {m.group(1)} closed, remove TODO?" # CI/presubmit mein chalao; PR khud mat merge karo, human review rakho

FDE / interview angle

  • Client ke legacy monolith (jaise HSBC migration) pe agent lagane se pehle 'agent-readiness' pass: stale TODOs, dead modules, outdated README/specs saaf karo, warna tokens aur scope creep dono badhenge.
  • Metrics bolo: steps/session, tokens/session, out-of-scope diffs ka % — Google ne inhi se impact naapa.
  • Brownfield mein agent ko golden example files do (BCE / airails card ki tarah) taaki wo achha pattern imitate kare.

Self-check

  1. Stale TODO human ke liye harmless par agent ke liye costly kyun hai? Google ke numbers se samjhao.
  2. Brownfield repo mein agent ka output quality improve karne ke 3 concrete steps kya honge?
AI software factory on Kubernetes (Tekton + Claude Code + in-cluster vLLM) + Kargo promotion, IDPs in agent era, K8s picksHistorical@piotr_minkowski @NaveenS16 @kubetools · DevOps / K8s / Cloud · 8:16 AM IST (11 Nov)GitHub webhook → Tekton → agent pipeline → same-cluster vLLM; deploy ko bundle ki tarah promote karo; confroll, Kaniko, hardened images.

@piotr_minkowski @NaveenS16 @kubetools · DevOps/K8s · 8:16 AM IST (11 Nov)

Sources: https://x.com/piotr_minkowski/status/2120264371876020648 · https://x.com/NaveenS16/status/2120210343087775998 · https://x.com/NaveenS16/status/2120212928557457524 · https://x.com/kubetools/status/2120250750714695899 · https://x.com/kubetools/status/2120250916414877889

Post kya kehta hai

  • @piotr_minkowski: 'AI software factory' jo poori Kubernetes pe chalti hai — issue/PR khole ya comment kare to GitHub webhook Tekton ko hit karta hai, Tekton context kheench ke pipeline mein Claude Code agent start karta hai, aur agent same cluster mein chal rahe vLLM model se baat karta hai.
  • @NaveenS16: Kargo se continuous promotion — deployment ko bundle (app images, charts, configs) ki tarah treat karo, loose container tags chase mat karo; multi-stage rollouts safe (talk: https://youtu.be/eb_rf1lFe0A).
  • @NaveenS16: 'IDPs Are Dead' — portals/service catalogs ki jagah devs AI coding agents se infra generate, PR open, deployments trigger karte hain (https://youtu.be/kfqmqnnf85g).
  • @kubetools RT @kubezilla_io: confroll CLI — ConfigMap/Secret change pe pods restart na hone wali problem (kubernetes#22368). RT @learnk8s: Kaniko bina Docker daemon ke container ke andar Dockerfile se image build.

Deep analysis

  • Factory ka shape = event-driven CI: webhook → pipeline → agent step → PR. Tekton ka fayda: har agent run ek isolated pod/task, logs + retries + RBAC K8s se milte hain. In-cluster vLLM: code/data cluster se bahar nahi jata (regulated clients ke liye bada point), par GPU capacity aur model quality ki zimmedari tumhari.
  • Kargo ka idea: 'freight' = ek tested combination (image + chart + config) jo stage-by-stage promote hota hai. Tag drift (dev mein v1.2, prod mein galti se latest) khatam; rollback bhi bundle level pe.
  • IDPs dead claim: interface badal raha hai (portal → agent), par golden paths, policies, guardrails abhi bhi platform team ke — agent unhi APIs ko call karega. Isliye 'platform as API' zyada important.
  • confroll problem: K8s ConfigMap update pe running pods env vars refresh nahi karte; common fix checksum annotation ya Reloader-type controller. Kaniko: daemonless build = privileged Docker socket mount ki zarurat nahi (security win).

Mini flow (agent pipeline steps)

  1. Trigger — GitHub issue/PR/comment webhook → Tekton EventListener
  2. Context — Task repo clone + issue text + relevant files collect
  3. Agent — Claude Code container, model endpoint = in-cluster vLLM service
  4. Verify — Tests/lint task; fail ho to agent ko feedback ke saath dobara
  5. Output — PR open; human review; merge ke baad Kargo freight promote

FDE / interview angle

  • Bank client bole 'code bahar nahi jaana chahiye' — ye pattern (self-hosted model + K8s pipeline) seedha answer hai; trade-off: GPU cost, model quality, ops burden.
  • Spring Boot microservices ke liye: image + Helm chart + application.yaml config ek freight mein, staging se prod promotion — 'works in staging, breaks in prod' kam.

Java 28: Primitive Patterns preview withdraw, Constant Patterns aayenge (Valhalla numerics ki wajah se) + Lombok vs RecordsHistorical@starbuxman (→ amber-spec-experts) @piotr_minkowski · Java / Spring / JPA · 8:16 AM IST (11 Nov)5 previews (JDK 23–27) ke baad feature JDK 28 se hata; exact numeric conversion Valhalla ke naye numeric types se align hona chahiye.

@starbuxman → amber-spec-experts · Java · 8:16 AM IST (11 Nov)

Sources: https://x.com/starbuxman/status/2120242311397347575 (→ https://mail.openjdk.org/archives/list/amber-spec-experts@openjdk.org/thread/UYEK3PGOHN6R56AHJTSLPGAY363K666S/) · https://x.com/piotr_minkowski/status/2120262812933276078

Bummed the primitive patterns preview is being dropped in Java 28, but excited about where things might go instead— @starbuxman

Post kya kehta hai

  • Angelos Bimpoudis + Gavin (9 Nov 2026, amber-spec-experts): 'Primitive Types in Patterns, instanceof, and switch' ka development band. Sixth preview ka draft JEP withdraw, feature JDK 28 se remove — --enable-preview ke saath bhi nahi milega.
  • History: JDK 23 (JEP 455), 24 (JEP 488), 25 (JEP 507), 26 (JEP 530), 27 (JEP 532) mein preview.
  • Wajah: Valhalla nayi Java numerics explore kar raha hai, user-defined numeric types bhi; unhe exact numeric conversion support karna padega. Valhalla abhi evolve ho raha hai, to current definition finalize karna premature.
  • Aage: Constant Patterns — kuch constants ko patterns ki tarah, nested record patterns mein bhi, e.g. case Point(0, 0); kuch when clauses hat jayenge. Draft JEP jaldi. JEP 12 bina naye JEP ke JBS issue se preview removal allow karta hai.

Deep analysis

  • Exact numeric conversion = pattern tabhi match kare jab conversion lossless ho (int 42 byte mein fit, 1000 nahi). Ye safety feature tha; problem ye ki jab naye numeric types (Valhalla value classes) aayenge, unke liye bhi 'exact' ka matlab same rules se define hona chahiye — warna language mein do inconsistent definitions.
  • Preview process ka asli matlab yahi hai: preview features final nahi hote, aur 5 previews ke baad bhi withdraw ho sakte hain. Production code mein preview features pe dependency = upgrade risk.
  • Lombok vs Records (RT, @cat_edelveis video): records immutable data carriers ke liye @Value/@Data ki jagah le sakte hain; par builders, mutable JPA entities jaisi jagah Lombok/handwritten code abhi bhi (post sirf video title deta hai).

Mini example (Java)

// JDK 23–27 preview (JDK 28 mein remove): int x = 1000; if (x instanceof byte b) { /* exact check: 1000 byte mein fit nahi => false */ } // Bina preview, aaj ka safe tarika: if (x >= Byte.MIN_VALUE && x <= Byte.MAX_VALUE) { byte b = (byte) x; } // Proposed Constant Patterns (draft JEP aana baaki, syntax final nahi): record Point(int x, int y) {} String where(Object o) { return switch (o) { case Point(0, 0) -> "origin"; // mail ka example case Point p -> "elsewhere"; default -> "unknown"; }; }

FDE / interview angle

  • Interview: 'preview feature production mein use karoge?' — nahi; ye case example do (5 previews ke baad withdraw). Enterprise/bank mein LTS + final features hi.
  • Modern Java story: records + sealed types + pattern matching se Lombok/visitor boilerplate kam; Valhalla value classes performance side ka next step (Vector API card bhi isi ka intezaar kar raha hai).

Quick picks (8:16 AM, 11 Nov)

  • @vlad_mihalcea https://x.com/vlad_mihalcea/status/2120207514155303177 — Hypersistence Optimizer 2.15 release; 18 Nov free webinar ke baad newsletter subscribers mein 50 one-year licenses giveaway.
  • @starbuxman RT @dashaun https://x.com/starbuxman/status/2120208412088709291 — GatewayJUG St. Louis talk: 'OAuth stands for fun!'.

Self-check

  1. Exact numeric conversion kya hai, aur Valhalla ke user-defined numeric types ki wajah se ise finalize karna premature kyun tha?
  2. Constant Patterns se switch mein when clauses kaise kam hote hain? case Point(0, 0) ka example samjhao.
LLM apps in production: OpenTelemetry observability (FastAPI), AI vuln-triage harness steering file, AgentCore Web Search + voice/fintech-security picksHistorical@freeCodeCamp @VKazulkin @Prathkum · AI / Agents & LLMs · 8:16 AM IST (11 Nov)Retrieval, prompt, model call, tokens, cost, eval ko trace karo; vuln triage ko steering file se consistent banao; diarization bina notes galat.

@freeCodeCamp @VKazulkin @Prathkum · AI/LLMs · 8:16 AM IST (11 Nov)

Sources: https://x.com/freeCodeCamp/status/2120201975857914027 · https://x.com/VKazulkin/status/2120209622326001855 · https://x.com/VKazulkin/status/2120216284423983572 · https://x.com/VKazulkin/status/2120217293984542733 · https://x.com/Prathkum/status/2120263024762327288 · https://x.com/swapnakpanda/status/2120218264626491550

Post kya kehta hai

  • @freeCodeCamp: LLM apps ko basic monitoring se zyada chahiye — FastAPI mein OpenTelemetry se end-to-end tracing: retrieval, prompt building, model calls, token usage, cost, evaluation signals.
  • @VKazulkin (AWS Security blog): 'Configuring your AI vulnerability harness, Part 2: The steering file' — AI model se structured, evidence-based vulnerability triage, seasoned analyst jaisi consistency ke saath.
  • @VKazulkin: Bedrock AgentCore InvokeWebSearch API — apps, custom agent loops, data pipelines ko search results, koi resource create kiye bina. CloudWatch Logs account-level retention policies — platform/security teams sab log groups ko standard pe la sakti hain.
  • @Prathkum: AI meeting notes aksar isliye fail hote hain ki speech-to-text 'I'll ship it Friday' galat speaker pe daal deta hai — diarization (kisne kya bola) skip karna; koi prompt ise fix nahi karta. @swapnakpanda: Live 3 voice — baat karte waqt sunta rehta hai, 'yeah' pe continue, 'wait, actually' pe ruk jata hai.

Deep analysis

  • LLM observability = normal APM + LLM-specific attributes. Ek request ka trace: retrieve span → prompt span → llm.call span (input/output tokens, model, latency, cost) → eval span. Isse pata chalta hai slow answer retrieval ki wajah se hai ya model ki, aur cost kis feature pe ja raha hai.
  • Steering file idea: triage instructions, evidence format, severity rules ek versioned file mein — har run same rubric, isliye output consistent aur auditable. Ye wahi 'harness engineering' trend hai (InfoQ trends card).
  • Diarization point general lesson hai: pipeline ka upstream error (ASR speaker label) downstream LLM prompt se theek nahi hota. Eval upstream stages ka bhi chahiye.
  • Managed web search API = agent ko tool dena bina scraper infra ke; trade-off: vendor lock-in aur search results bhi untrusted input (prompt injection) hain.

Mini example (Python: FastAPI + OpenTelemetry spans)

from opentelemetry import trace tracer = trace.get_tracer("rag-api") @app.post("/ask") def ask(q: Question): with tracer.start_as_current_span("rag.request") as span: with tracer.start_as_current_span("retrieve"): docs = retriever.search(q.text, k=5) with tracer.start_as_current_span("llm.call") as s: resp = llm.generate(build_prompt(q.text, docs)) s.set_attribute("llm.tokens.input", resp.usage.input_tokens) s.set_attribute("llm.tokens.output", resp.usage.output_tokens) span.set_attribute("rag.docs", len(docs)) return {"answer": resp.text} # Spring AI side: Micrometer observations model calls + token usage export karti hain

FDE / interview angle

  • Client demo ke baad pehla sawaal 'cost kitna aur kyun?' — per-request token + cost tracing ready rakho. Spring AI mein Micrometer/OTel se yahi dikhao.
  • Fintech: Claude personal finance features pe TechRepublic ka sawaal (kitna financial data AI ko dena chahiye) — data minimisation, masking, audit logs, retention policy (CloudWatch account-level jaisi) client ko propose karo.

Quick picks (8:16 AM, 11 Nov)

Deep analysis

  • Common thread: guardrails prompt mein nahi, server/platform par. LinkedIn ka delete-protection aur peer approval wahi pattern hai jo bank mein maker-checker hota hai — agent ko tool deta waqt API layer par hi policy lagao.
  • DoorDash ka point important hai: LLM judge tabhi bharosemand hai jab human labels se calibrate ho, aur offline + online dono par same rubric chale. Warna offline gains prod mein dikhte hi nahi.
  • Limitation: yeh conference preview hai, talks ke andar ka detail abhi public nahi; numbers speakers ke claims hain.

Mini example (Java)

// Server-side guardrail for an agent tool (Spring)
@PostMapping("/ops/delete")
public ResponseEntity<?> delete(@RequestBody DeleteReq r, @RequestHeader("X-Agent-Id") String agent) {
  if (r.env().equals("prod") && !approvals.hasPeerApproval(r.changeId()))
    return ResponseEntity.status(403).body("peer approval required");
  rateLimiter.acquire(agent);
  return ResponseEntity.ok(ops.delete(r));
}

FDE / interview angle: Customer ke saath agent deploy karte waqt pehla sawaal: 'agent kis identity se chalega aur kaunsa action irreversible hai?' Wahan approval + rate limit server par lagao, aur launch se pehle eval rubric customer ke SMEs se calibrate karwao.

JioHotstar ne coding agents ke liye 6 code-retrieval approaches benchmark kiyeKaam ka: High@InfoQ · AI / Agents & LLMs · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta)Koi ek retrieval sabse best nahi: concrete tasks par structural search (Sourcebot), ambiguous requirements par semantic RAG; filesystem tools baseline strong raha.

@InfoQ · AI / Agents & LLMs · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Tum CodeGraph jaisa code-graph/RAG kaam karte ho — yeh real benchmark batata hai ki retrieval layer kab madad karti hai aur kab sirf tokens jalati hai. · Kya karna hai: read fully — aur apne code-graph tool ko filesystem baseline ke against isi tarah measure karo

  • @InfoQ
    https://x.com/InfoQ/status/2120524501213687948

Post kya kehta hai: InfoQ news: JioHotstar ne AI coding agents ke knowledge bases benchmark kiye.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • JioHotstar Benchmarks Knowledge Bases for AI Coding Agents (InfoQ, Leela Kumili) — Abhishek Bansal (JioHotstar) ne 6 approaches compare kiye: plain filesystem tools, Sourcebot, Graphify, CocoIndex Code, Repowise, aur in-house RAG. SWE-bench subsets, CodeScaleBench aur apne codebase ke Q&A harness par; solver Claude Sonnet. Metrics: accuracy, tokens, agent turns, tool usability. Results: filesystem baseline concrete tasks par competitive; Sourcebot (indexed text search + symbol defs/refs) SWE-bench Verified ke harder tasks par baseline se better aur sasta; SWE-bench Lite par retrieval ne mostly overhead add kiya. CodeScaleBench par ambiguous descriptions ke liye semantic retrieval zyada helpful. Extra retrieval layers ne consistently accuracy ya token cost improve nahi kiya. Agents familiar ops (text search, file read, symbol lookup) prefer karte the. Recommendation: filesystem baseline rakho, concrete ke liye structural search, ambiguous/cross-cutting ke liye semantic index, aur tool-use traces bhi measure karo.
    https://bit.ly/3VeqraV

Deep analysis

  • Sabse bada lesson: 'RAG add kar do' default answer nahi hai. Agent ko grep/read_file/find_symbol jaise simple, predictable tools pasand hain; high-level abstraction jisko verify karna pade, woh kam use hoti hai.
  • Benchmark caveat (article mein bhi): probabilistic models par single-run numbers noisy hote hain — multiple runs ka cost/accuracy chahiye. Subsets chhote the.

Mini example (Python)

# Minimal eval harness idea: same task, different toolsets
for toolset in ['fs_only', 'fs+symbols', 'fs+semantic']:
    for task in tasks:
        for run in range(3):  # repeat: LLMs are noisy
            r = agent.solve(task, tools=TOOLS[toolset])
            log(toolset, task.id, r.passed, r.tokens, r.turns, r.tool_calls)

@bibryam · AI / Agents & LLMs · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Banking/payments mein agent-initiated transactions ka fraud, dispute aur KYC problem seedha tumhare domain (HSBC, WU) se juda hai — FDE conversations mein yeh aayega. · Kya karna hai: read fully — challenge table (friendly fraud, traffic commingling, KYC) ko apne payments context se map karo

  • @bibryam
    https://x.com/bibryam/status/2120475876814967112

Post kya kehta hai: Bilgin Ibryam ne Cloudflare blog share kiya: 'Agents aren't humans (but they kinda are)'.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • Agents aren't humans (but they kinda are) — Cloudflare Blog (Bryan Becker, 5 Nov 2026) — Hosted agents intermediary hain: site ko agent dikhta hai, user nahi, aur ek agent kai users ke liye act karta hai. 'Intermediation Principle': user-site ka business relationship aur security responsibility intermediary ke baawajood bani rehni chahiye. Challenge table: account sharing, compliance, friendly fraud (user agent ki purchase dispute kare), CAPTCHA irrelevant, identity spoofing, KYC/liveness break, misalignment, resource exhaustion, risk-based auth break, traffic commingling (ek abusive user ki wajah se poora agent block), unauthorized card users. HITL (CAPTCHA user ko pass karna) aur narrow scopes ko insufficient bataya. Proposal: Web Bot Auth (agent requests sign kare), MOLE (bina user ID ke rate limiting, Privacy Pass ka successor), OAuth for account identity, identity spectrum (nothing/session/account/unique), claims (18+, cardholder), machine-readable agent access policy. Trusted Agent Delegation (TAD) coalition announce. Cloudflare customers ke liye: Security Analytics mein Personal Assistants category, aur Account Abuse Protection mein Hashed User ID par WAF rule.
    https://blog.cloudflare.com/trusted-agent-delegation

Deep analysis

  • Core insight: authorization scope ('agent kya kar sakta hai') aur accountability ('galat hua to kaun jawab dega') alag cheezein hain. Payments mein dispute ka cost site uthati hai, isliye merchant/bank side par agent identity signals critical hain.
  • Ye abhi standards-in-progress hai (IETF, coalition); production-ready universal solution nahi. Claims ko verified facts ke saath confuse mat karo.

FDE / interview angle: Bank customer ke liye agentic checkout ya ops agent banate waqt: agent ki signed identity, user ka account binding (OAuth), aur har action ka audit trail with correlation ID — yeh teen cheezein design doc mein pehle din se rakho.

AI software factory: Review bottleneck nahi, risk hai (Patrick Debois) + controls khud approve na kar sake (jbaruch)Kaam ka: Medium@bibryam, @jbaruch · AI / Agents & LLMs · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta)Har diff padhne ki jagah: likelihood kam karo, outcome verify karo, risk locate karo, impact contain karo — autonomy ek risk-budget decision hai.

@bibryam, @jbaruch · AI / Agents & LLMs · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Agentic SDLC ka mental model clear karta hai; FDE/lead interviews mein 'AI se PRs ki baadh kaise sambhaloge' ka strong answer deta hai. · Kya karna hai: read fully — 4 controls yaad rakho

  • @bibryam
    https://x.com/bibryam/status/2120449956385312976
  • @jbaruch
    https://x.com/jbaruch/status/2120535885049692426

Post kya kehta hai: Bilgin ne Tessl blog share kiya. Baruch Sadogursky (AI DevCon NYC talk clip) kehte hain: software factory apne hi controls hatane ko approve na kar sake; controls aur spending allowance badalna fully human control mein rahe.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • Review Isn't the Bottleneck. Risk Is. (Tessl, Patrick Debois, 28 Oct 2026) — Factory PRs itni tezi se banata hai ki review queue badhti hai, par asli sawaal: bina har line padhe change aage kab ja sakta hai? 4 controls: (1) likelihood kam karo — tests, static analysis, policy checks, enforceable architecture constraints, adversarial reviewer agents jo genuinely alag kaam karein, isolated sandboxes; (2) outcome verify karo — acceptance scenarios, independent verification agent, intent se linked evidence bundle; (3) risk locate karo — criticality, data sensitivity, dependency centrality, incident history, reversibility (data migration revert nahi hoti); (4) impact kam karo — cells, circuit breakers, feature flags, progressive exposure, rate limits, tested rollback. Autonomy specific decisions par grant ho (edit, merge, expose, data modify), risk budget ke hisaab se. Jbaruch ka video clip maine dekha nahi (video hai).
    https://tessl.io/blog/review-isnt-the-bottleneck-risk-is

Deep analysis

  • Payment authorization ki ek-line change internal dashboard ke bade refactor se zyada risky hai — isliye same release policy sab par mat lagao.
  • Jbaruch wala point Debois se judta hai: agent ko apne guardrails modify karne ki permission dena = control plane collapse.

FDE / interview angle: Customer ko AI coding rollout pitch karte waqt: 'har PR review karenge' scale nahi hota; risk tiers define karo aur high-risk paths par human approval + evidence bundle.

Self-check

  • Debois ke 4 controls kaunse hain?
  • Reversibility risk classification mein kyun matter karti hai?

@freeCodeCamp · AI / Agents & LLMs · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Python + agent orchestration hands-on practice ke liye accha starter, FDE prep ke liye directly useful. · Kya karna hai: try — freeCodeCamp par tutorial dhoondh ke weekend mein local Ollama ke saath banao

  • @freeCodeCamp
    https://x.com/freeCodeCamp/status/2120442115872248060

Post kya kehta hai: Post: LLM prompts chaotic ho jaate hain jab ek hi baar plan, write, review karna ho; Darsh ka tutorial Python + LangGraph mein multi-agent system banata hai, Ollama aur Qwen use karke.

Deep analysis

  • Planner/writer/reviewer split = separation of concerns; har node ka prompt chhota aur testable. LangGraph state graph mein edges se loop (review fail to rewrite) bana sakte ho.
  • Local Qwen via Ollama = free experimentation, par quality cloud models se kam ho sakti hai.

Mini example (Python)

from langgraph.graph import StateGraph, END
g = StateGraph(dict)
g.add_node('plan', plan); g.add_node('write', write); g.add_node('review', review)
g.set_entry_point('plan')
g.add_edge('plan', 'write'); g.add_edge('write', 'review')
g.add_conditional_edges('review', lambda s: 'write' if s['needs_fix'] else END)
app = g.compile()

FDE / interview angle: Spring AI mein yahi pattern advisors/chain se banta hai; interview mein dono stacks (LangGraph vs Spring AI) compare kar paana plus point hai.

Self-check

  • Multi-agent split se kya fayda aur kya cost (latency, tokens) aata hai?

Ghanton chalne wali APIs: Oracle MicroTx Workflows + TxEventQ se reliable event-driven flowKaam ka: High@OracleDevs · Java / Spring / JPA · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta)202 Accepted + durable workflow, versioned events (eventId/correlationId/causationId), layered idempotency, ambiguous payment outcome ka explicit handling.

@OracleDevs · Java / Spring / JPA · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Payments/fintech backend ka core design (idempotency, lost reply, duplicate event) — tumhare WU/HSBC experience aur interviews dono se seedha juda. · Kya karna hai: read fully — failure scenarios 1-3 interview answers ki tarah yaad karo

  • @OracleDevs
    https://x.com/OracleDevs/status/2120537036947939801

Post kya kehta hai: Post: hours tak chalne wali APIs ke liye MicroTx aur TxEventQ se async processing, durable messaging, transaction coordination.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • When APIs Run for Hours: Building Reliable Event-Driven Workflows with Oracle MicroTx and TxEventQ (Oracle blog, Brijesh Deo, 21 Oct 2026) — Insurance claim example: POST /claims turant 202 + claimId + operationId; Assessment workflow (validate, AI analysis, fraud check, human review) approval persist karke claim.approved event publish karta hai; event handler Settlement workflow start karta hai. Event ko async API maano: eventType, eventVersion, eventId (dedupe), correlationId (poori journey), causationId (direct trigger). Failures: (1) client response lose — Idempotency-Key persist karo, retry same operation return kare; (2) event do baar — eventId dedupe + workflow-start idempotency + payment ko business key (claim-98371:settlement); (3) payment success par reply lost — call timeout, retry budget aur business deadline alag; same idempotency key se retry ya pehle status query; warna compensation/investigation/human. AI output untrusted input: validate, evidence persist, deterministic policy decide kare; payments deterministic controls ke peeche. Ops: business states (SUBMITTED...SETTLED), deadline breach / backlog / unknown payment par alerts, runbooks.
    https://social.ora.cl/6015BHZes3

Deep analysis

  • 'Exactly once' ka wada nahi — at-least-once maan ke har layer idempotent banao. Yeh Kafka world mein bhi same hai (consumer dedupe + downstream idempotency key).
  • Timeout != failure. Lost reply par blind retry = double payment; isliye status query ya same idempotency key. Vendor-specific (Oracle) tooling hai, par pattern Temporal/Camunda/Kafka saga mein bhi lagta hai.

Mini example (Java)

@PostMapping("/claims")
public ResponseEntity<Op> submit(@RequestHeader("Idempotency-Key") String key, @RequestBody Claim c) {
  Op op = ops.findByKey(key).orElseGet(() -> ops.start(key, c)); // same key -> same operation
  return ResponseEntity.accepted().location(URI.create("/operations/" + op.id())).body(op);
}

@KafkaListener(topics = "claim.approved")
void onApproved(ClaimApproved e) {
  if (!processed.add(e.eventId())) return; // dedupe
  payments.pay(e.claimId() + ":settlement", e.amount()); // business idempotency key
}

FDE / interview angle: Customer ka 'payment double ho gaya' incident — FDE ko lost-reply scenario, idempotency key aur reconciliation runbook samjhana aana chahiye. AI step ko deterministic envelope mein rakhna bank customers ko convince karta hai.

Self-check

  • eventId, correlationId aur causationId mein kya fark hai?
  • Payment call timeout ho jaye to blind retry kyun galat hai aur sahi steps kya hain?
Vlad Mihalcea: Batch processing best practicesKaam ka: Medium@vlad_mihalcea · Java / Spring / JPA · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta)Batches mein poll karo, stateless thread-safe processor, throttling with hysteresis, results persist karo, external APIs flood mat karo, EIP style.

@vlad_mihalcea · Java / Spring / JPA · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Purana (2013) par timeless batch/scheduler rules; Spring batch jobs aur interviews mein kaam ke. · Kya karna hai: read fully — 10 minute ka article

  • @vlad_mihalcea
    https://x.com/vlad_mihalcea/status/2120518106426523989

Post kya kehta hai: Vlad ne apna batch processing best practices article share kiya.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • Batch processing best practices (vladmihalcea.com, Oct 2013) — Input-processor-output components. Rules: hamesha batch mein poll karo (scheduler 2 din band raha, sab items ek saath load karke OOM); processor stateless aur thread-safe, local job context; queues par throttling — consumers ko queue size par auto-scale, create threshold kill threshold se bada (jitter avoid); job results memory ki jagah persistent store mein; external service ko loop mein flood mat karo, thread sleep ki jagah EIP Delayer; EIP tools (queues, polling channels, splitters/aggregators, delayers) aur Spring Integration recommend.
    https://vladmihalcea.com/scheduled-jobs-best-practices/

Deep analysis

  • Batch mein poll karna = backpressure ka simplest form. Tests chhote data par pass hote hain, prod backlog par OOM — classic.
  • Create/kill threshold alag rakhna (hysteresis) wahi idea hai jo KEDA/HPA cooldown mein hai.

Mini example (Java)

@Scheduled(fixedDelay = 5000)
void run() {
  List<Item> batch;
  do {
    batch = repo.findPending(PageRequest.of(0, 500)); // never findAll()
    batch.forEach(processor::process);
  } while (batch.size() == 500);
}

FDE / interview angle: Customer ka nightly job OOM kar raha hai — pehla check: kya woh sab pending rows ek saath load kar raha hai?

Self-check

  • Create aur kill thresholds barabar kyun nahi hone chahiye?
JPA/Hibernate: child badle to parent (aggregate root) ka version kaise badhayeinKaam ka: Medium@vlad_mihalcea · Java / Spring / JPA · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta)RootAware interface + Hibernate FlushEntity/Persist event listeners jo root par OPTIMISTIC_FORCE_INCREMENT lock lete hain.

@vlad_mihalcea · Java / Spring / JPA · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Aggregate-level optimistic locking banking entities (account + transactions) mein real problem hai; deep Hibernate knowledge. · Kya karna hai: read fully — code GitHub par hai, ek baar run karke dekho

  • @vlad_mihalcea
    https://x.com/vlad_mihalcea/status/2120422576950292813

Post kya kehta hai: Vlad ka article: jab bhi child entity modify ho, parent entity ka version increment kaise karein.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • How to increment the parent entity version whenever a child entity gets modified with JPA and Hibernate (2016) — Post (root, @Version), PostComment, PostCommentDetails; children RootAware<Post> implement karte hain. FlushEntityEventListener dirty/deleted child detect karke session.lock(root, OPTIMISTIC_FORCE_INCREMENT) karta hai; PersistEventListener naye child par same. Integrator se register, hibernate.integrator_provider property. SQL output dikhata hai: child update ke saath UPDATE post SET version = n+1 WHERE version = n. Comments mein Vlad: @ManyToOne/child-side @OneToOne best perform karte hain; JPA entities ko DDD aggregates ke liye use karne par caution.
    https://vladmihalcea.com/how-to-increment-the-parent-entity-version-whenever-a-child-entity-gets-modified-with-jpa-and-hibernate/

Deep analysis

  • Default optimistic locking sirf usi row ko protect karta hai; aggregate invariant (jaise total limit across children) ke liye root version bump zaroori.
  • Har child change par root UPDATE = extra write aur contention; hot aggregates par conflicts badhenge. Hibernate 6 mein listener APIs check karna.

Mini example (Java)

public interface RootAware<T> { T root(); }
// in FlushEntityEventListener.onFlushEntity:
if (entity instanceof RootAware r && updated(event))
    event.getSession().lock(r.root(), LockMode.OPTIMISTIC_FORCE_INCREMENT);

FDE / interview angle: Concurrent edits se aggregate invariant toot raha ho (do users ek hi order ke items badal rahe) — yeh pattern ya explicit root lock suggest karo.

Self-check

  • OPTIMISTIC vs OPTIMISTIC_FORCE_INCREMENT mein kya fark hai?

CPU-based HPA hata ke KEDA: request rate, connections aur consumer lag par scalingKaam ka: Medium@K8sArchitect · DevOps / K8s / Cloud · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta)I/O-bound pods 20-30% CPU par bhi saturated the; KEDA Prometheus triggers se request rate, active connections aur Pulsar lag par scale kiya.

@K8sArchitect · DevOps / K8s / Cloud · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Kafka consumers ko lag par scale karna tumhare stack ka direct production concern hai. · Kya karna hai: read fully — ScaledObject YAML ek baar kind cluster par try karo

  • @K8sArchitect
    https://x.com/K8sArchitect/status/2120534770467635639

Post kya kehta hai: Case study: CPU-based HPA ko KEDA se replace kiya, request rate, connection pressure aur queue depth par scaling.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • Why We Replaced Kubernetes HPA with KEDA (Medium, nutcas3, Sep 2026) — API pods 30% CPU par 500 pending GraphQL queries aur DB pool exhausted; collectors 500 connection cap par ~20% CPU par connections drop; worker single replica, Pulsar lag ka alert raat 2 baje. KEDA ScaledObjects: API sum(rate(http_requests_total[1m])) threshold 100 + CPU 70 fallback; collector avg(device_connections_active) threshold 400 (500 cap se pehle); worker sum(pulsar_consumer_lag) threshold 100. KEDA: Prometheus direct query, scale to zero, 15s polling. GitLab CI se deploy, purane HPA delete. Alerts: ScalerNotReady, MaxedOut, OperatorDown, ConsumerLagHigh. Results (author claims): p99 flat, rejected connections 0, workers 12 tak auto-scale. Takeaway: CPU lagging indicator hai.
    https://ku.bz/93WBc_br6

Deep analysis

  • Kafka ke liye KEDA ka native kafka scaler bhi hai (consumer group lag); par replicas partitions se zyada karna bekaar — extra consumers idle rahenge.
  • Article mein numbers author ke hain; '30s HPA loop hardcoded' jaise claims verify karne layak.

Mini example (YAML)

triggers:
- type: kafka
  metadata:
    bootstrapServers: kafka:9092
    consumerGroup: payments-consumer
    topic: payments
    lagThreshold: "100"

FDE / interview angle: Customer ka consumer lag badhta hai par HPA scale nahi karta — answer: CPU galat signal hai, lag par scale karo, max replicas = partitions.

  • Kafka consumer replicas partitions se zyada kyun nahi hone chahiye?

Saved: Vlad Mihalcea — Clustered Index beginner guideKaam ka: Medium@vlad_mihalcea · Saved posts · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta)Clustered index = PK B+Tree jiske leaf nodes mein poori row; secondary index leaf mein PK; compact aur monotonic PK (UUID nahi) behtar.

@vlad_mihalcea · Saved posts · 11 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · DB internals interview mein pakka aate hain; UUID PK wala point real performance issue hai. · Kya karna hai: read fully

  • @vlad_mihalcea
    https://x.com/vlad_mihalcea/status/2018233023922106394

Post kya kehta hai: Vlad ka clustered index guide (saved post).

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • Clustered Index (vladmihalcea.com, 2021) — B+Tree basics (sab keys leaf mein, linked leaves range scan ke liye). Clustered index = tree-organized table, MySQL aur SQL Server ka default. Secondary index leaf mein PK value store karta hai; extra column chahiye to Bookmark Lookup (logical reads 2 se 4); SQL Server INCLUDE se covering index. PK compact rakho (int vs bigint) kyunki har secondary index mein copy hota hai. Monotonic PK: random UUID se page splits, low fill factor, buffer pool misses; TSID suggest.
    https://vladmihalcea.com/clustered-index/

Deep analysis

  • PostgreSQL default heap table hai (clustered nahi) — yeh fark interview mein bolna accha signal hai.

CREATE NONCLUSTERED INDEX IDX_Post_Title ON Post (Title) INCLUDE (CreatedOn);

Tests as data: AI ke code ke liye customer tests contract banaoKaam ka: High@martinfowler · AI / Agents & LLMs · 12 Nov 2026, 9:00 AM IST (Asia/Calcutta)Matteo Vaccari (Thoughtworks) kehte hain: jab AI code + tests dono likhe, to human-readable customer tests ko test data files mein rakho taaki agent unhe chupke se na badle.

@martinfowler · AI / Agents & LLMs · 12 Nov 2026, 9:00 AM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Agents se code likhwana FDE ka daily kaam hai; trust kaise banaye ye interview aur customer dono mein poocha jaata hai. · Kya karna hai: read fully — article padho aur ek Spring service ke liye CSV-driven acceptance test try karo

  • @martinfowler
    https://x.com/martinfowler/status/2120538201551200467

Post kya kehta hai: Martin Fowler ne Matteo Vaccari ka article share kiya: agentic engineering mein testing strategy ko broader-scope tests ki taraf le jaana chahiye jo code ke bajaye data ke roop mein ho.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • Tests as data (Matteo Vaccari, 11 Nov 2026) — Teen claims: (1) jab AI code likhta hai, developer uska customer ban jaata hai; (2) isliye customer tests (developer tests nahi) human aur AI ke beech primary contract hain; (3) ye contract test code ke bajaye text/data files mein rakho taaki coding agent use unexpectedly modify na kare. Early 2026 mein 26 Thoughtworks developers ke interviews: zyada tar ne kaha ki AI code ke saath tests bhi refactor kar deta hai, isliye tests pe bharosa nahi. Proposal: black-box system/component tests, tabular parameterized tests, jahan table na chale wahan ad hoc test DSL, data files code se alag, aur AI-generated test runner. Fit/specification by example, Go table tests aur approval testing pe based. Saath mein mutation testing, property-based tests, lints aur manual exploratory testing bhi chahiye.
    https://martinfowler.com/articles/tests-as-data.html

Deep analysis

  • Core idea: agent ke paas tests edit karne ki power hai, to tests 'source of truth' nahi rehte. Data files (CSV/YAML/approved logs) ko alag folder mein rakh ke protect karna (CODEOWNERS, pre-commit check, agent instructions) ek guardrail ban jaata hai.
  • Trade-off: DSL aur custom runner pehle mehenge the; author ka point hai ki ab AI unhe sasta bana deta hai. Limitation: har test table mein fit nahi hota, article khud 'limits of applicability' section deta hai.
  • Java world mein ye JUnit 5 @CsvFileSource / parameterized tests aur approval testing se seedha map hota hai.

Mini example (Java)

// src/test/resources/fx-quotes.csv (human-owned, agent edit na kare)
// from,to,amount,expected
@ParameterizedTest
@CsvFileSource(resources = "/fx-quotes.csv", numLinesToSkip = 1)
void quote(String from, String to, BigDecimal amt, BigDecimal expected) {
    assertEquals(expected, fxService.quote(from, to, amt));
}

FDE / interview angle: Customer ke saath acceptance criteria ko table/data file mein likhwao: business wale padh sakte hain, aur agent-generated code us contract ke against verify hota hai. Interview mein 'AI-generated code ko production-safe kaise banaoge' ka strong jawab.

Self-check

  • Tests ko data files mein rakhne se coding agent ke tests-badalne wale problem mein kaise madad milti hai?
  • Is approach ke saath kaunse complementary techniques article recommend karta hai?

Generated Glossary: LLM se company ki jargon dictionary banwaoKaam ka: High@martinfowler · AI / Agents & LLMs · 12 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta)Specialized domain ke terms LLM ke training set mein nahi hote; ek LLM se org ke docs/chats se glossary generate karwao, experts review karein, phir baaki LLMs ko context mein do.

@martinfowler · AI / Agents & LLMs · 12 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · FDE ka pehla kaam hi customer ki domain language samajhna hai; banking jaise domain (HSBC, FX, interbank) mein ye seedha apply hota hai. · Kya karna hai: try — apne current project ke 30-40 domain terms ka LLM-generated glossary bana ke agent ke context mein daalo

  • @martinfowler
    https://x.com/martinfowler/status/2120884699794911385

Post kya kehta hai: Specialized domain mein bahut terms LLM ke training data mein nahi hote; mitigation: ek LLM se glossary generate karwao jo dusre LLMs context mein use karein.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • Generated Glossary (Martin Fowler bliki, 12 Nov 2026) — Sources: org ke documents, emails, chat logs (zarurat ho to audio/video transcripts). Generator naye terms aur 'known terms jo unfamiliar tarah use ho rahe hain' dono dhoondhe (doosre wale sabse zyada confusion karte hain). Har term ki dictionary-style definition, multiple meanings ho to context ke saath. Human domain experts review karein. Glossary version control mein, scheduled refresh. DDD bounded contexts: ek single standard glossary banane se bacho, har context ka alag glossary; source code bhi ek bounded context hai aur code-derived vs human-text glossary compare karna instructive hai. Bada glossary ho to pehle sirf terms + links context mein do, full definition model zarurat pe padhe. Harness ke sensors generated code/UI mein sahi vocabulary check kar sakte hain; 'mat use karo, ye use karo' aur deprecated terms bhi rakho; internal terms customer-facing UI mein na jaayein.
    https://martinfowler.com/bliki/GeneratedGlossary.html

Deep analysis

  • Ye RAG ka sasta, high-leverage version hai: poora knowledge base nahi, sirf vocabulary layer. Tokens kam, aur agent ke naming/answers customer ki language mein aate hain.
  • Limitation: LLM galat definition bana sakta hai, isliye expert review mandatory hai; aur ek hi word alag teams mein alag matlab rakhta hai (e.g. settlement, booking), to per-context glossary chahiye.
  • Lazy loading pattern (terms + links, definition on demand) wahi idea hai jo skills/MCP resources mein hota hai: context window ko pehle se mat bharo.

Mini example (Java)

// Spring AI: glossary ko system context mein inject karo
String glossary = Files.readString(Path.of("glossary/payments.md"));
String answer = chatClient.prompt()
    .system(s -> s.text("Use these domain terms exactly as defined: {g}").param("g", glossary))
    .user(question)
    .call().content();

FDE / interview angle: Customer site pe pehle hafte ka deliverable: LLM-generated + SME-reviewed glossary. Interview mein bolo ki domain onboarding ko aise systematically agent context mein badla.

Self-check

  • Glossary ke liye 'known term used in unfamiliar way' kyun sabse risky hai?
  • Single company-wide glossary ke bajaye per-bounded-context glossary kyun?

Mini example (Java)

@Tool(description = "Analyze a slow SQL query")
Analysis analyze(String sql) {
  var plan = planner.explain(sql);
  return new Analysis(plan.summary(),
     List.of("next: call suggestIndexes(sql)", "next: call rewriteQuery(sql)"));
}

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • (When) Should a Software Factory be Lights-off? Benchmarking Code Review Agents on Bug Detection — CRBugBench: repo history se bugs mine karke unke introducing PR ke saath review task; 1,000 tasks 98 open-source GitHub repos se + 800 ek badi company ke proprietary repos se. Best (claude-opus-5 with copilot-cli) top-5 comments mein 71.9% bugs pakadta hai, paanch mahine pehle opus-4.6 ka 47.7% tha. Agents complex changes aur non-local reasoning wale bugs pe kamzor, proprietary repos pe sharp drop (wahan project context zyada chahiye). Compute scale karne mein sabse zyada fayda prompt changes se: exploration-nudging prompts se +5.8% at ~10% zyada cost. Guidance: review effort PR complexity ke hisaab se, project priorities se steer karo, repository knowledge do, PR type ke hisaab se model choose karo, model badalne pe quality monitor karo.
    https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13668
  • InfoQ: Panel: Who Builds the Frontend Now? (And What Breaks When They Do) — InfoQ post ka link is QCon London panel pe khula (AI tools, vibe coding, AI-generated code review fatigue, agents ke liye systems, guardrails). Post mein jo Claude-writes/Gemini-reviews workflow bataya hai, uska exact hissa transcript mein dhoondha nahi gaya.
    https://bit.ly/4kADIVn

Deep analysis

  • Sabse important finding: proprietary repos pe drop. Matlab customer codebase pe agent ko context (architecture docs, conventions, glossary) dena padega, warna benchmark numbers mislead karenge.
  • Writer aur reviewer alag model rakhne ka idea correlated blind spots kam karta hai; paper bhi PR characteristics ke hisaab se model dispatch karne ko kehta hai.
  • 71.9% ka matlab ~28% bugs abhi bhi miss; isliye fully lights-off abhi risky hai, high-risk PRs pe human review rakho.

FDE / interview angle: Customer poochega 'kya hum human review hata dein?' Jawab: risk-tiered review, repo context do, aur apne PRs pe chhota eval chala ke measure karo.

Self-check

  • Review agents proprietary repos pe kyun kamzor hain?
  • Exploration-nudging prompt kya hota hai aur iska cost/benefit kya tha?

  • Which Claude model should you build with? Fable, Opus, Sonnet, or Haiku (Addy Osmani, 9 Nov 2026) — Lineup: Haiku 5.5, Sonnet 5.5, Opus 5.5, Fable 5.2 (Mythos 5.2 verification programs ke through). TL;DR: Opus 5.5 default effort medium; Fable 5.2 se aadhi per-token cost. Sonnet 5.5 scoped bug fix/spec work, Haiku 5.5 classification/routing/extraction aur subagents, Fable hardest projects ya long unattended runs. Effort ek finer dial hai; high ke upar ek level bade model jitna mehenga ho sakta hai; effort model ko nayi knowledge nahi deta. Multi-model system: har request strictest model ke hisaab se banao (append-only history, tool_choice auto with strict tools, content blocks type se padho), routing simple ho to code se model pick karo. Haiku 4.5 se 5.5 migration: budget_tokens ki jagah effort, non-default temperature/top_p/top_k pe 400. Measure: real tasks + outcome check, 2-3 effort levels, cost vs score plot, winner ko shadow traffic pe chalao.
    https://claude.dev/blog/which-claude-model-should-you-build-with/

Deep analysis

  • Code se routing, model se nahi: ek achha production rule hai; deterministic, sasta, testable.
  • Score-vs-spend plot wala approach generic eval discipline hai jo kisi bhi provider pe lagta hai; model names badlenge, method nahi.
  • Ye vendor ka apna blog hai, to recommendations unke models ke favour mein hongi; apne data pe verify karo.

Mini example (Java)

// Code-based routing (Spring AI): model se routing mat karwao
ChatClient pick(TaskType t) {
  return switch (t) {
    case CLASSIFY, EXTRACT -> haikuClient;
    case SCOPED_FIX -> sonnetClient;
    default -> opusClient; // medium effort
  };
}

  • Effort level badhana aur bada model lena kab equivalent nahi hote?
  • Multi-model setup kab rakhna justified hai?

Deep analysis

  • Long-running agent = long-running workflow: checkpointing, idempotent steps, resume-from-step, retries ke bina 95% kaam waste. Ye wahi Kafka/Saga wali soch hai jo Vivek already jaanta hai.
  • LLM gateway (LiteLLM jaisa) enterprise mein standard pattern ban raha hai: per-team budget, fallback provider, caching; claims post author ke experience pe hain, verify nahi kiye.

client = OpenAI(base_url="http://localhost:4000", api_key="team-key")
r = client.chat.completions.create(model="default-chat", messages=[{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}])
print(r.choices[0].message.content)
