    https://x.com/gunnarmorling/status/2120834958256132373

Post kya kehta hai: Vlad ne apne do purane articles re-share kiye. Saath mein Java Code Geeks: StructuredTaskScope task lifecycles handle karta hai jab ek concurrent task fail ho; Gunnar Morling ka RT: IO aur CPU mix ho to in-flight fetches ke liye virtual threads aur decoding ke liye fixed pool (RT text truncated, link nahi).

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • The best way to do batch processing with JPA and Hibernate (2017) — Loop mein persist; har batchSize (e.g. 25) pe transaction commit, naya begin, aur entityManager.clear(). Fayde: long transactions nahi (MVCC ke liye kharab), failure pe pichhle batches safe, persistence context chhota (memory aur flush speed). Exception pe rollback zaroori warna locks atke reh sakte hain. Config: hibernate.jdbc.batch_size=25, hibernate.order_inserts=true, hibernate.order_updates=true; 50 inserts sirf 2 batched JDBC executions mein gaye.
    https://vladmihalcea.com/the-best-way-to-do-batch-processing-with-jpa-and-hibernate/
  • Spring read-only transaction Hibernate optimization (2018) — Spring 5.1 se pehle readOnly=true sirf flush mode MANUAL karta tha. Vlad ke PR (SPR-16956) ke baad read-only flag Hibernate Session tak jaata hai, to entities read-only mode mein load hoti hain aur loaded (hydrated) state discard hota hai; ye state dirty checking aur versionless optimistic locking ke liye hota hai. Manually: session.setDefaultReadOnly(true) ya query hint org.hibernate.readOnly. Fayda: read-heavy methods mein memory bachat.
    https://vladmihalcea.com/spring-read-only-transaction-hibernate-optimization/

Deep analysis

  • Batch mein clear() bhoolna classic OOM/slow-flush bug hai; aur IDENTITY id generation Hibernate JDBC insert batching disable kar deta hai (ye article mein nahi, general Hibernate behaviour hai; SEQUENCE use karo).
  • readOnly=true sirf documentation nahi hai: dirty checking snapshot hatta hai. Read-only queries pe lagao, write paths pe nahi.
  • Note: dono articles purane (2017/2018) hain; Spring Boot mein property spring.jpa.properties.hibernate.jdbc.batch_size se set hoti hai.

Mini example (Java)

// application.properties
// spring.jpa.properties.hibernate.jdbc.batch_size=25
// spring.jpa.properties.hibernate.order_inserts=true
@Transactional(readOnly = true)
public List<Txn> report(LocalDate d) { return repo.findByDate(d); }

FDE / interview angle: Legacy monolith migration (HSBC jaisa) mein bulk jobs aur read-heavy reports yahi do knobs se fast hote hain; customer demo mein before/after SQL count dikhana strong proof hai.

Self-check

  • Batch job mein entityManager.clear() na karne se kya do problems aati hain?
  • Spring 5.1+ mein readOnly=true Hibernate ke liye extra kya karta hai?

llm-d: Kubernetes pe distributed LLM inference aur KV-cache aware routingKaam ka: High@NaveenS16 · AI / Agents & LLMs · 13 Nov 2026, 9:00 AM IST (Asia/Calcutta)Normal K8s load balancer LLM ke KV/prefix cache ko waste karta hai; llm-d (vLLM family, Red Hat) cache locality aur load dono balance karke GPU utilization badhata hai.

@NaveenS16 · AI / Agents & LLMs · 13 Nov 2026, 9:00 AM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Inference cost/latency customer deployments ka core issue hai; FDE ko serving stack ke trade-offs bolne aane chahiye. · Kya karna hai: read fully — InfoQ podcast ke Key Takeaways + llm-d section padho

  • @NaveenS16
    https://x.com/NaveenS16/status/2121074783706337623
  • @NaveenS16
    https://x.com/NaveenS16/status/2121073541642846618

Post kya kehta hai: Pehla post: traditional K8s load balancers blind routing karke LLM caching kharab karte hain, llm-d sessions sticky rakh ke conversational KV cache reuse karta hai aur load bhi spread karta hai. Doosra post InfoQ podcast share karta hai (Wasm backend, TornadoVM se Java on GPU, llm-d + vLLM).

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • Distributed LLM Inference on Kubernetes with llm-d, Wasm, & TornadoVM (InfoQ podcast, 9 Nov 2026, Edoardo Vacchi with Olimpiu Pop) — Key takeaways: Wasm backend runtime ban chuka hai (sandboxing, cross-language); TornadoVM Java code ko GPU backends pe compile/run karta hai (pure Java mein GPU-accelerated Llama 3 jaise projects); vLLM single instance pe PagedAttention se throughput deta hai, llm-d isko multi-node K8s clusters tak le jaata hai; llm-d same instance pe cached context reuse aur node overload ke beech balance karta hai; roadmap: idle time mein batch/scheduled inference aur semantic classification se prompt ko sahi size ke model pe route karna.
    https://www.infoq.com/podcasts/distributed-llm-inference-kubernetes/

Deep analysis

  • Mechanism: LLM request ka cost prefill (prompt process) mein bahut hota hai; agar same conversation ka agla turn usi pod pe jaaye jahan KV/prefix cache pada hai to prefill bachta hai. Round-robin LB ye locality tod deta hai.
  • Trade-off: pure stickiness hot-spot banata hai (ek GPU overload), isliye llm-d cache hit aur load dono score karke route karta hai.
  • Semantic routing (chhota vs bada model) wahi cost-control idea hai jo LLM gateway mein model routing se hota hai; batch inference idle GPU ko use karta hai.

Mini example (Python)

# Concept: cache-aware routing score (illustrative)
def pick_pod(pods, prefix_hash):
    def score(p):
        hit = 1.0 if prefix_hash in p.cached_prefixes else 0.0
        return 0.7 * hit - 0.3 * p.queue_depth / p.max_queue
    return max(pods, key=score)

FDE / interview angle: Customer bolta hai 'GPU mehenge hain, latency high hai': pehle poochho prompts mein shared prefix kitna hai, multi-turn hai ya nahi, phir cache-aware routing + prefix caching suggest karo.

Self-check

  • Round-robin load balancing LLM serving mein KV cache reuse kyun todta hai?
  • vLLM aur llm-d ka scope kaise alag hai?

€49.99 ka invoice €4,999 charge hua: AI agents ke saath Conway's LawKaam ka: High@milan_milanovic · AI / Agents & LLMs · 13 Nov 2026, 9:00 AM IST (Asia/Calcutta)Billing rewrite mein teams apne agents se baat karti rahin, ek-doosre se nahi; invoicing cents bhejta tha, payments euros padhta tha. @milan_milanovic · AI / Agents & LLMs · 13 Nov 2026, 9:00 AM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Payments domain + AI-built microservices ki real failure story; Vivek ke HSBC migration aur FDE dono ke liye seedha relevant. · Kya karna hai: read fully — section 5 (AI) aur 'name the force' steps

    https://x.com/milan_milanovic/status/2120909518695698863

Post kya kehta hai: Invoicing cents bhejta tha, payments euros padhta tha, har team apne agent se baat karti thi; Conway's Law dono taraf AI ke saath.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

(Tech World With Milan, 12 Nov 2026) — 12 saal purane billing system ka rewrite: CTO ne PoC ke baad 3 mahine bola, 140 special cases fail hue, token board lagaya (Goodhart), 5 naye engineers + 10 AI agents, 40 microservices, month 9 mein ship par sabse bade customer ka import toota. (Note: X ke link-preview ne 404 dikhaya tha, par direct fetch kaam kiya.)
    https://newsletter.techworld-with-milan.com/we-promised-three-months-it-took

Deep analysis

  • Bug technical nahi, contract ka hai: money ke liye unit aur currency explicit type mein honi chahiye, integer minor units + ISO currency code.
  • AI agents code fast likhte hain par cross-team agreement nahi banate; API contract (OpenAPI/schema + contract tests) hi woh jagah hai jahan humans ko milna chahiye.
  • Modular monolith pehle, phir jo module zyada badle use extract karo: legacy migration (HSBC type) mein yahi safe path hai.

Mini example (Java)

// Money ko kabhi raw double/long mat bhejo
public record Money(long minorUnits, Currency currency) {
    public static Money ofMajor(String amount, String code) {
        Currency c = Currency.getInstance(code);
        long minor = new BigDecimal(amount)
            .movePointRight(c.getDefaultFractionDigits())
            .longValueExact();
        return new Money(minor, c);
    }
}
// Money.ofMajor("49.99", "EUR") -> 4999 minor units, EUR

FDE / interview angle: Customer ke system mein agent integrate karte waqt pehla sawaal: amounts kis unit mein hain aur contract kaun own karta hai? Ye story interview mein 'agentic SDLC ke risks' pe strong example hai.

  • Is cents/euros bug ko contract test kaise pakadta?
  • Gall's Law AI-generated microservices pe kaise apply hota hai?

False sharing: ek padded field se 5x tak speed farakKaam ka: Medium@javacodegeeks · Java / Spring / JPA · 13 Nov 2026, 9:00 AM IST (Asia/Calcutta)Do threads alag fields likhte hain par same 64-byte cache line share hoti hai to line cores ke beech bounce karti hai; padding/@Contended/LongAdder se 5-8x tak sudhaar published tests mein.

@javacodegeeks · Java / Spring / JPA · 13 Nov 2026, 9:00 AM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Java concurrency interview classic hai aur high-throughput payment counters mein kaam aata hai, par AI focus se door. · Kya karna hai: try — article ka FalseSharingDemo multicore machine pe chalao

  • @javacodegeeks
    https://x.com/javacodegeeks/status/2120951037624283450

Post kya kehta hai: Article title share kiya: False Sharing and Cache Lines.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • False Sharing and Cache Lines: One Padded Field, a Measurable Slowdown (Java Code Geeks, 12 Nov 2026) — Cache line aam taur pe 64 bytes; MESI coherence mein write ke liye exclusive ownership chahiye. Adjacent volatile long fields wale test: Arm Learning Path median 15.5s se 2.9s (~5.4x), m0wfo demo 53.7s se 7.0s (~7.7x), JMH 148.5M se 728.6M ops/s (~4.9x). Cost: @Contended ne object 32 se 288 bytes kiya (default 128 bytes padding dono taraf), JDK ke bahar -XX:-RestrictContended chahiye; ek case mein padded slower bhi mila, isliye measure karo. Options: @Contended, array mein har 16th slot, LongAdder. Checklist mein Linux perf c2c bhi.
    https://www.javacodegeeks.com/2026/11/false-sharing-and-cache-lines-one-padded-field-a-measurable-slowdown.html

Deep analysis

  • Counters ke liye LongAdder sabse practical hai; sum() atomic snapshot nahi deta.
  • Padding memory badhata hai, millions of objects pe GC cost.

Mini example (Java)

// Per-thread hot counter ke liye
LongAdder processed = new LongAdder();
processed.increment();      // threads alag cells mein likhte hain
long total = processed.sum(); // approximate snapshot

FDE / interview angle: Performance issue mein 'threads badhaye par throughput flat' dikhe to false sharing ek hypothesis rakho.

Self-check

  • False sharing mein data share nahi hota phir bhi slow kyun?

OKE par inference-aware routing: Istio + Gateway API Inference Extension + llm-dKaam ka: High@OracleDevs · AI / Agents & LLMs · 13 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta)LLM traffic ko round-robin ki jagah model/pool/KV-cache signals ke basis par route karne ka hands-on walkthrough.

@OracleDevs · AI / Agents & LLMs · 13 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Inference serving aur routing FDE customer deployments ka core hai; Kubernetes-native LLM routing ka concrete pattern. · Kya karna hai: read fully — InferencePool, EPP aur HTTPRoute ka flow diagram khud banao

  • @OracleDevs
    https://x.com/OracleDevs/status/2121260552777245046

Post kya kehta hai: Post kehta hai ki round-robin LLM ke liye purana hai; OKE par Istio, Gateway API Inference Extension aur llm-d mila ke CPU aur GPU inference pools ke beech ek hi endpoint ke peeche smart routing hoti hai.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • Configure advanced inference ingress on OCI Kubernetes Engine (OKE) with the Gateway API Inference Extension — Oracle blog (Kay Singh, 5 Oct 2026). Normal load balancer ko pata nahi hota kaunsa model maanga gaya, server kitna busy hai, ya prompt ka part cache hai. Demo: customer-support app, Standard (CPU) aur Fast (NVIDIA A10 GPU) do pools, dono same model Gemma 4 E2B serve karte hain. Layers: Gateway API (Gateway/HTTPRoute, OKE managed Istio implement karta hai), GAIE (InferencePool resource + endpoint picker protocol), llm-d Router (EPP — pool ke andar compatible vLLM server chunta hai), llm-d Inference Payload Processor (OpenAI request ke model field se X-Gateway-Base-Model-Name header lagata hai). InferenceModelRewrite alias ko asli model naam mein badalta hai. Article saaf kehta hai ki demo sirf pool selection dikhata hai, ek pool ke andar load-based replica selection nahi; Standard/Fast OKE performance tiers nahi hain.
    https://social.ora.cl/6016BHLpnI

Deep analysis

  • Key idea: LLM request ka cost aur latency backend state (queue depth, KV-cache locality) par depend karta hai, isliye L7 routing ko model-aware banana padta hai.
  • Same model ko CPU aur GPU pool par rakhna latency-sensitive (live customer) vs background (ticket summary) traffic ka sasta split hai.
  • Limitation: demo mein har pool mein ek hi endpoint hai, toh EPP ka asli faayda (KV-cache aware replica choice) yahan prove nahi hota.

FDE / interview angle: Customer bolega 'GPU mehenga hai' — tum CPU/GPU pool split + model-alias routing propose kar sakte ho, ek OpenAI-compatible endpoint ke saath. Interview mein 'LLM gateway kaise design karoge' ka strong answer.

Self-check

  • InferencePool aur normal Kubernetes Service mein kya fark hai?
  • IPP header kyun add karta hai, HTTPRoute seedha model field kyun nahi padh sakta?

  • GitHub Makes Agentic Workflows Generally Available with Isolation and Budget Controls - InfoQ — Mark Silvester, 11 Nov 2026. Open-source project; tasks jaise issue triage aur CI failure analysis.   • @InfoQ
    https://x.com/InfoQ/status/2121241169581818271

Post kya kehta hai: Google ne AQuA (Ambient Quality Agent) open source kiya jo production AI agent failures diagnose karta hai.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • Google Open Sources Ambient Quality Agent (AQuA) to Diagnose Production AI Agent Failures - InfoQ — AQuA agent ke request path ke bahar chalta hai, Cloud Trace / Cloud Logging / BigQuery se telemetry padhta hai, per run 1,000 tak recent sessions sample karta hai. 5 stages: sampling, reviewing, clustering findings, verifying clusters, tracking issues. Quality criteria `goal.md` mein, deterministic custom metrics bhi. Root cause ke liye deployment par liya immutable source snapshot use karta hai, file aur line ranges cite karta hai; fixes ya PR khud nahi karta. Travel app demo: 32 sessions, 42 findings, 9 clusters, verifier ne 3 reject kiye, 6 verified (jaise seat availability check bypass, vegan preference delegation mein khona). Prompt fix + replay ke baad seat bypass 15 se 2, dietary omissions 7 se 0 — yeh controlled replay tha, production nahi. Findings NEW/RECURRING/RESOLVED (14 din na dikhe toh). Limits: sampling rare failures miss kar sakta hai, replay external state reproduce nahi karta.
    https://bit.ly/4C1jXwC

Deep analysis

  • Infra monitoring 200 OK dikhata hai jabki agent ne user preference ignore kar di — isliye semantic quality monitoring alag layer hai.
  • Verifier stage (false-positive clusters reject karna) LLM-as-judge ki reliability problem ka practical jawab hai.
  • Root cause ko deployed snapshot ki line numbers se bandhna hallucinated diagnosis rokta hai.

FDE / interview angle: Customer ka agent 'kabhi kabhi galat' karta hai — tum trace-based sampling + clustered findings + replay ka loop propose kar sakte ho. Interview: 'agent ko production mein kaise evaluate karoge?'

  • Controlled replay ke results production improvement kyun guarantee nahi karte?
LLM Batch API ka asli hisaab: 50% discount x waiting trafficKaam ka: High@arpit_bhayani · AI / Agents & LLMs · 13 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta)Batch API saving = 50% x jitna traffic 24 ghante wait kar sakta hai; pehle model tier, phir caching, phir batch.

@arpit_bhayani · AI / Agents & LLMs · 13 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · LLM cost optimisation customer conversations mein turant aata hai; yeh ordering yaad rakhne layak hai. · Kya karna hai: read fully — post ka order (tier, cache, batch) ek cost-estimate sheet mein apply karo

  • @arpit_bhayani
    https://x.com/arpit_bhayani/status/2121089628744667359

Post kya kehta hai: Post ke mutabik batch APIs ~50% sasti hoti hain par response 24 ghante tak le sakti hain; 60% traffic eligible ho toh 30% bachat, 20% ho toh sirf 10%. Batch discount cache discount ke baad bache spend par lagta hai, isliye output-heavy workloads ko zyada faayda. Order: pehle sahi capability tier (real-time Haiku batch Sonnet se sasta ho sakta hai), phir caching, phir dekho kya wait kar sakta hai. Use cases: tickets classify karna, documents summarise, evals chalana, synthetic data.

Deep analysis

  • Yeh claims post ke author ke hain; exact pricing provider ke rate card par verify karna.
  • Practical: evals aur nightly backfills batch ke natural candidates hain, user-facing chat nahi.
  • Model downgrade + caching often batch se bada lever hai.

Mini example (Python)

def effective_saving(eligible_share, batch_discount=0.5):
    return eligible_share * batch_discount

print(effective_saving(0.6))  # 0.3 -> 30%

FDE / interview angle: Client bolega 'LLM bill zyada hai' — tum workload ko latency classes mein baant ke tier/caching/batch plan doge. Strong FDE talking point.

Self-check

  • Agar 40% traffic batch-eligible hai toh rate card ke 50% discount se effective bachat kitni?
  • Caching on karne se batch ka relative faayda output tokens par kyun shift hota hai?

Hibernate: har Persistence Context ka alag JDBC batch sizeKaam ka: Medium@vlad_mihalcea · Java / Spring / JPA · 13 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta)Session.setJdbcBatchSize() se use case ke hisaab se batch size, global hibernate.jdbc.batch_size ki jagah.

@vlad_mihalcea · Java / Spring / JPA · 13 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Java/Spring backend interviews aur bulk payment inserts mein seedha kaam aata hai; article purana (2016) hai. · Kya karna hai: read fully — 5 min; apne bulk insert code mein try karo

  • @vlad_mihalcea
    https://x.com/vlad_mihalcea/status/2121151526013984831

Post kya kehta hai: Vlad ne apna article share kiya: Hibernate mein har Persistence Context ka JDBC batch size customise karna.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • How to customize the JDBC batch size for each Persistence Context with Hibernate (27 Sep 2016) — hibernate.jdbc.batch_size global setting har business use case par lagti hai. Hibernate 5.2 se `entityManager.unwrap(Session.class).setJdbcBatchSize(10)` session-level setting deta hai. Example: 20 Post entities, batch size 10 se 2 database roundtrips instead of 20. Article mein Hypersistence Optimizer ka promo bhi hai.
    https://vladmihalcea.com/how-to-customize-the-jdbc-batch-size-for-each-persistence-context-with-hibernate/

Deep analysis

  • Bulk job (jaise end-of-day settlement) ko bada batch, normal request ko chhota/default — ek hi app mein.
  • Batching ke liye IDENTITY id generation insert batching disable karta hai — yeh article mein nahi, alag se yaad rakho (general Hibernate knowledge).

Mini example (Java)

@Transactional
public void importPosts(List<Post> posts) {
    em.unwrap(Session.class).setJdbcBatchSize(50);
    posts.forEach(em::persist);
}

FDE / interview angle: HSBC migration mein bulk data load par DB roundtrips kam karna — measurable win.

Self-check

  • setJdbcBatchSize kis object par call hota hai?
  • 20 entities aur batch 10 par kitne roundtrips?

SkillsJars: Agent Skills Maven Central par, ab CLI aur 'Skills over MCP' bhiKaam ka: High@JamesWard · AI / Agents & LLMs · 14 Nov 2026, 9:00 AM IST (Asia/Calcutta)SkillsJars agent skills ko JAR ki tarah Maven Central se version karta hai; naya sjm CLI aur ek no-auth MCP server har skill serve karta hai, Spring AI SkillsTool ke saath classpath se seedha.

@JamesWard · AI / Agents & LLMs · 14 Nov 2026, 9:00 AM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Java/Maven dev ke liye skills ka sabse natural packaging; Spring AI agent mein skills plug karne ka direct code path. · Kya karna hai: try — Spring AI app mein spring-ai-agent-utils + ek SkillsJar add karke SkillsTool wire karo

  • @JamesWard
    https://x.com/JamesWard/status/2121345048419709398

Post kya kehta hai: James Ward ne announce kiya ki SkillsJars mein ab CLI aur MCP (Skills over MCP) add ho gaye.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • SkillsJars - Docs — SkillsJars = Agent Skills packaged as JARs on Maven Central (versioning, transitive grouping, no copy-paste). Security warning: skills malicious ho sakte hain, basic scan review ka substitute nahi. Maven/Gradle/sbt extraction plugin skills ko assistant ke folder mein nikalta hai (e.g. `./mvnw skillsjars:extract -Ddir=.kiro/skills`). Naya `sjm` CLI bina JVM/build tool ke install karta hai, skillsjars.json mein versions pin karta hai. Skills over MCP: stateless Streamable HTTP server https://www.skillsjars.com/mcp, no auth; MCP Skills extension clients `skills/list`, `skills/get` use karte hain, baaki `resources/list`/`resources/read`; sirf latest version serve hota hai. Spring AI: org.springaicommunity:spring-ai-agent-utils 0.5.0 ka SkillsTool classpath:/META-INF/skills se skills padhta hai, ChatClient ke defaultToolCallbacks mein. Apne skills publish: skills/<name>/SKILL.md + maven-plugin package goal.
    https://www.skillsjars.com/docs

Deep analysis

  • Teen distribution models: build-time extraction (pinned), sjm CLI (non-JVM), MCP (always latest, zero setup). Prod ke liye pinned, exploration ke liye MCP.
  • Skills ko dependency banana supply-chain risk bhi laata hai — docs khud kehte hain vet karo; enterprise mein internal Maven repo + review gate chahiye.
  • Spring AI mein skills progressive disclosure jaisa kaam karte hain: tool ke through on-demand SKILL.md load, poora context nahi bharta.

Mini example (Java)

@Value("${agent.skills.paths}") List<Resource> skillPaths; // classpath:/META-INF/skills

ChatClient chat = builder
    .defaultToolCallbacks(SkillsTool.builder()
        .addSkillsResources(skillPaths).build())
    .build();

FDE / interview angle: FDE angle: customer ke internal runbooks ko SkillsJar bana ke unke Nexus/Artifactory se ship karo — Java shops ke liye familiar governance.

Self-check

  • Skills over MCP mein version pinning kyun nahi milti, aur prod mein kya karoge?
  • Spring AI mein SkillsTool kis path se skills load karta hai?

@OracleDevs · Java / Spring / JPA · 14 Nov 2026, 9:00 AM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Banking (HSBC jaisa) clients mein passwordless DB auth real requirement hai; Java-specific aur seedha kaam ka, AI se kam juda. · Kya karna hai: read fully — sirf 'Choose a token acquisition path' table aur UCP refresh FAQ

  • @OracleDevs

Post kya kehta hai: Post: OCI aur Entra token files, SDK suppliers, JDBC providers aur connection pools ka complete runnable guide, DB setup aur Entra identity config ke saath.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • Passwordless Java Connections to Oracle AI Database with OCI IAM and Microsoft Entra ID — Oracle blog (Paul Parkinson, 26 Oct 2026). OCI IAM mein JDBC token ke saath private key ka possession prove karta hai; Entra mein DB resource ke liye access token. Database identity ko authorized schema par map karta hai. Deep analysis

  • Sabse useful insight: pool mein purana authenticated session token expiry ke baad bhi chalta hai, bug tab dikhta hai jab naya physical connection banta hai — load test mein connection churn force karo.
  • OCI PoP token aur Entra bearer JWT ka format alag; mix mat karo.
  • Same pattern Postgres/Azure AD ya AWS IAM DB auth par bhi lagta hai — concept transferable.

FDE / interview angle: Regulated customer ke saath 'no static DB passwords' ek common security ask; FDE ko identity → schema mapping aur pool refresh risk samjhana aana chahiye.

Self-check

  • Token-based DB auth mein refresh failure normal testing mein kyun chhup jaata hai?

Long-running LangGraph agents: SQS queue + K8s workers + KEDA scale-to-zeroKaam ka: High@K8sArchitect · AI / Agents & LLMs · 14 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta)Agent jobs ko sync HTTP se hata ke queue → worker pods → Postgres checkpoint pe chalao, KEDA queue length pe scale kare.

@K8sArchitect · AI / Agents & LLMs · 14 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Production agent deployment ka exact FDE pattern hai, aur Kafka/queue background wale Java dev ke liye directly map hota hai. · Kya karna hai: read fully — phir Spring Boot + Kafka consumer + DB checkpoint ke saath same pattern ka chhota POC try karo

  • @K8sArchitect
    https://x.com/K8sArchitect/status/2121620686716260466

Post kya kehta hai: Tutorial: long-running LangGraph agents ko Kubernetes workers ki tarah chalao, SQS se jobs, progress PostgreSQL mein save, aur KEDA queue length ke basis pe scale.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • Scaling Long-Running AI Agents: Async Workers on Kubernetes with LangGraph and KEDA (Othman Bricha, Medium, 1 Jul 2026) — AWS GenAI infra series ka part 3. Problem: Supervisor/Worker graph kai LLM + tool calls chain karta hai, Lambda ki 15-min limit aur open HTTP connection fragile. Fix: API Gateway → Lambda sirf SQS pe enqueue karke 202 Accepted + job_id return karta hai; EKS worker pods SQS long-poll (WaitTimeSeconds=20) karte hain, LangGraph graph PostgresSaver checkpointer ke saath compile hota hai, thread_id = job_id — pod evict/OOM ho to retry pe last checkpoint se resume. Result DynamoDB/S3 mein. KEDA ScaledObject (aws-sqs-queue trigger): minReplica 0, max 20 (Bedrock quota + Postgres connections bachane ke liye), pollingInterval 15s, queueLength 5 (~1 pod per 5 messages), cooldownPeriod 60s. CPU HPA galat hai kyunki workers Bedrock ka wait karte hain, CPU nahi jalate. Warnings: SQS at-least-once hai — checkpointer graph state bachata hai, tool side-effects (email, 3rd-party write) ko alag idempotency key chahiye; DLQ lagao; per-tenant cost metrics; 0→20 burst pe Postgres connection pool spike; EKS control-plane cost (Fargate/Step Functions alternative); noisy-neighbour tenants ke liye per-tenant queues. Last line: yeh sab ab kaafi had tak Amazon Bedrock AgentCore managed form mein deta hai.
    https://medium.com/@othman.bricha/scaling-long-running-ai-agents-async-workers-on-kubernetes-with-langgraph-and-keda-e4d09555969d

Deep analysis

  • Core idea: 'submit' aur 'execute' ko decouple karo. Agent run minutes le sakta hai, isliye 202 + job_id + polling/notification sahi contract hai — yeh wahi async-request-reply pattern hai jo tum payments mein use karte ho.
  • Checkpoint ≠ exactly-once. LLM state resume ho jayegi, par agar tool ne transfer/email bhej diya aur message delete se pehle crash hua, retry pe dobara fire hoga. Banking context mein yeh sabse bada interview point hai: tool calls ke liye idempotency key (job_id + step).
  • Scaling signal choose karna: I/O-bound LLM workers ke liye queue depth/lag sahi metric hai, CPU nahi. Kafka mein equivalent KEDA kafka trigger (consumer lag) hoga.
  • Trade-off honestly likha hai: chhote workloads ke liye K8s overkill ho sakta hai; managed AgentCore build-vs-buy discussion ka natural next step hai.

Mini example (Java)

@KafkaListener(topics = "agent-jobs", groupId = "agent-workers")
public void onJob(AgentJob job, Acknowledgment ack) {
    // checkpoint key = jobId -> crash ke baad resume
    AgentState state = checkpoints.findById(job.id())
        .orElse(AgentState.start(job.prompt()));
    while (!state.done()) {
        Step step = planner.next(state);           // LLM call (Spring AI ChatClient)
        String key = job.id() + ":" + state.stepNo();
        if (!sideEffects.alreadyDone(key)) {       // idempotent tool call
            step.execute();
            sideEffects.markDone(key);
        }
        state = state.advance(step);
        checkpoints.save(state);
    }
    results.save(job.id(), state.output());
    ack.acknowledge();                              // commit offset last
}

FDE / interview angle: Customer bolega 'agent timeout ho raha hai' — tumhara jawab: sync gateway hatao, queue + resumable workers + DLQ + per-tenant metering. Interview mein at-least-once + idempotent tools + scale-on-lag teen points zaroor bolna.

Self-check

  • thread_id = job_id kyun rakha, aur yeh kis failure se bachata hai, kis se nahi?
  • Kafka pe yahi banana ho to KEDA kaunsa metric use karega aur offset kab commit karoge?
OpenShell (NVIDIA → CNCF Sandbox): AI agents ke liye sandbox + policy runtimeKaam ka: High@NaveenS16 · AI / Agents & LLMs · 14 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta)Agent ko untrusted code maano: filesystem, process, network aur credentials ko bahar ka supervisor control kare — K8s pe Agent Sandbox ke through.

@NaveenS16 · AI / Agents & LLMs · 14 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Enterprise (bank/HSBC jaisa) mein agent deploy karne ka pehla sawal security/governance hota hai; yeh uska concrete open-source answer hai. · Kya karna hai: read fully — dono CNCF posts padho; nirmata/demo-openshell repo ka naam yaad rakho, time ho to kind cluster pe try

  • @NaveenS16
    https://x.com/NaveenS16/status/2121588171922415695
  • @NaveenS16
    https://x.com/NaveenS16/status/2121586652904869910

Post kya kehta hai: Post 1: CNCF ne OpenShell ko Sandbox project ke roop mein accept kiya, NVIDIA ne contribute kiya — autonomous AI agents ko secure/govern karne ka runtime. Post 2: OpenShell on Kubernetes guide — har agent sandbox mein, bahar ka trusted supervisor decide karta hai agent kya access kar sakta hai.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • Welcoming OpenShell to the CNCF: Open Governance to Secure Agentic AI (CNCF, 10 Nov 2026) — KubeCon NA pe announcement (Jonathan Bryce CNCF, Ali Golshan NVIDIA). Point: agents ko real capability do bina data/secrets/network ka unchecked access diye; enforcement agent ke bahar infra layer mein. OpenShell agent behaviour manage/monitor karta hai aur policy boundaries enforce karta hai; policy language ke liye Cedar (CNCF project) adopt kar raha hai. Core contributors: NVIDIA, Red Hat, Docker, Canonical. Red Hat CTO Chris Wright ka quote: agents ke controls open aur auditable hone chahiye.
    https://www.cncf.io/blog/2026/11/10/welcoming-openshell-to-the-cncf-open-governance-to-secure-agentic-ai/
  • OpenShell on Kubernetes: A guide for platform engineers (CNCF, 11 Nov 2026, Jim Bugwadia, Nirmata) — Agents shell chalate, packages install karte, files padhte, network kholte hain — aksar internet se abhi uthaye text pe — isliye har agent 'untrusted code with real credentials' hai. YAML mein 4 controls: Filesystem (Landlock; demo mein /usr,/etc read-only, sirf /sandbox,/tmp writable), Process (non-root, no capabilities, seccomp), Network (default deny; per binary/host/port/HTTP method allow), Credentials (agent ko sirf placeholder key dikhti hai, supervisor real key sirf allowed endpoints ke liye swap karta hai). Apna scheduler nahi — K8s backend SIG Apps ka Agent Sandbox (agents.x-k8s.io) use karta hai, agents Pods/Services/PVCs/NetworkPolicies ban jaate hain. NetworkPolicy 'Pod api.github.com:443 tak ja sakta hai' bol sakti hai; OpenShell 'curl issues padh sakta hai par create nahi' bol sakta hai, aur running sandbox pe bina restart rule badal sakta hai. Demo: nirmata/demo-openshell, kind 2-node, K8s 1.35, Cilium 1.20, Agent Sandbox v1.0.5, OpenShell 0.1.3, Kyverno 1.18.2. (Main sirf page ka shuruati hissa padh paya.)
    https://www.cncf.io/blog/2026/11/11/openshell-on-kubernetes-a-guide-for-platform-engineers/

Deep analysis

  • Mental model: agent = untrusted plugin. Guardrails prompt mein nahi, infra mein — L7 egress policy (binary + method level) aur credential brokering sabse valuable idea hai.
  • Credential placeholder pattern seedha bank use-case pe map hota hai: LLM ko kabhi real API key/token nahi milna chahiye; supervisor/proxy inject kare.
  • Abhi Sandbox stage (0.1.x) hai — production pe lagane se pehle maturity dekhna; par concepts (Landlock, seccomp, default-deny egress, Cedar policies) interview mein bolne layak hain.

FDE / interview angle: Customer ka CISO puchhega 'agent ne galat API call kar di to?' — answer: sandboxed runtime, default-deny egress, per-endpoint credential injection, auditable policy (Cedar). Spring AI tool-calling app ke bahar bhi yeh layer lagani padti hai.

Self-check

  • NetworkPolicy aur OpenShell network rule mein exact farq kya hai?
  • Agent ko real API key kyun nahi dikhni chahiye, aur supervisor yeh kaise ensure karta hai?

JDBC batch update mein kaunsa statement fail hua — BatchUpdateException.getUpdateCounts()Kaam ka: Medium@vlad_mihalcea · Java / Spring / JPA · 14 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta)Batch fail hone pe getUpdateCounts().length batata hai kitne statements chale; agla wala culprit (driver/DB pe depend).

@vlad_mihalcea · Java / Spring / JPA · 14 Nov 2026, 9:00 PM IST (Asia/Calcutta) · Banking batch jobs/migrations mein seedha kaam aata hai; AI se nahi juda par core Java backend skill. · Kya karna hai: read fully — 5 min ka article, apne batch code mein exception handling check karo

  • @vlad_mihalcea
    https://x.com/vlad_mihalcea/status/2121614887084269613

Post kya kehta hai: Post apna purana article share karta hai: JDBC batch update mein failing statement kaise dhundein.

Links mein asal mein kya hai

  • How to find which statement failed in a JDBC Batch Update (21 Mar 2017) — Example: Post entity manually assigned id, PreparedStatement mein 5 INSERT batch, id = i % 2 taaki duplicate PK aaye. executeBatch() BatchUpdateException deta hai; e.getUpdateCounts() mein 2 entries → 2 statements chale, teesra fail. Conclusion: (1) datasource-proxy se JDBC statements log karo, (2) BatchUpdateException catch karke getUpdateCounts dekho. Comments mein Vlad ne kaha kaunse rows succeed hue yeh DB pe depend karta hai.
    https://vladmihalcea.com/how-to-find-which-statement-failed-in-a-jdbc-batch-update/

Deep analysis

  • Caveat: JDBC spec ke hisaab se driver fail ke baad baaki statements continue bhi kar sakta hai — tab array mein EXECUTE_FAILED (-3) entries aati hain. Isliye length aur values dono check karo.
  • Spring JdbcTemplate.batchUpdate mein yeh exception DataAccessException mein wrap ho jaata hai — root cause tak unwrap karna padta hai.

Mini example (Java)

try {
    jdbc.batchUpdate(sql, rows, 500, (ps, r) -> { ps.setLong(1, r.id()); ps.setString(2, r.title()); });
} catch (DataAccessException ex) {
    if (ex.getRootCause() instanceof BatchUpdateException bue) {
        int[] counts = bue.getUpdateCounts();
        for (int i = 0; i < counts.length; i++)
            if (counts[i] == Statement.EXECUTE_FAILED) log.warn("row {} failed", i);
        log.warn("processed {} before stop", counts.length);
    }
    throw ex;
}

FDE / interview angle: Customer data load fail ho to 'kaunsi row' turant batana trust banata hai — logging (datasource-proxy) + update counts.

Self-check

  • getUpdateCounts() ka length aur EXECUTE_FAILED value kya batate hain?
